# planner/mind.py —— L1 心智：纯文本聊天 + 一个 intention 工具。
#
# 设计（机主定稿）：
#   · 模型只输出文本 —— 正常聊天直接发到公屏，就像 ChatBot。
#   · 想动手就调 intention 工具，参数只有一句中文愿望；Python 把它交给 L2「许愿器」，
#     L2 翻译成步骤、L3 用 MineFlayer 执行。她在这期间照旧聊天。
#   · L2 的结果以普通消息注入上下文（「（身体）完成了：挖到 3 块圆石」），她据此再决定。
#   · 状态（坐标/血量/背包/附近/身体在做什么）与「小本本」（正在做什么/刚做过什么）
#     只作为一行附在本轮 user 消息后面，单槽临时，不进历史。
import asyncio
import json
import logging
import re
import time
from pathlib import Path

import config
import prompts
from budget import BudgetExceeded
from facts import extract_facts, facts_text, options_to_text
from l1 import Chain, Planner, SidecarClient, state_text
from llm import LLMClient, LLMError
from memory import MemoryStore
from notes import NoteStore
from schema import SchemaError, UnsupportedCapability

_logger = logging.getLogger("mind")


class _Log:
    """结构化一行日志：log.info("event", k=v)，和侧车的写法对齐。"""

    @staticmethod
    def _emit(level: int, event: str, **fields) -> None:
        tail = " ".join(f"{key}={value}" for key, value in fields.items())
        _logger.log(level, f"{event} {tail}".strip())

    def info(self, event: str, **fields) -> None:
        self._emit(logging.INFO, event, **fields)

    def warning(self, event: str, **fields) -> None:
        self._emit(logging.WARNING, event, **fields)

    def error(self, event: str, **fields) -> None:
        self._emit(logging.ERROR, event, **fields)


log = _Log()

DEFAULT_STATE = {
    "mode": "play",           # play / rest
    "rest_until": 0.0,
    "current": None,          # {"intention_id","text","priority","origin","at"}
    "note": "",               # 小本本：正在做 / 刚做过什么
    "next_tick_at": 0.0,
    "idle_rounds": 0,
    "last_say_at": 0.0,
}


def sanitize_say(text: str) -> str:
    """公屏只容得下一句话：去标记、压空白、截断，拦掉 JSON/代码味。"""
    if not text:
        return ""
    cleaned = str(text).strip()
    cleaned = re.sub(r"</?layout>.*?</layout>", "", cleaned, flags=re.DOTALL)
    cleaned = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", cleaned).strip()
    if not cleaned:
        return ""
    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    banned = ('{"', '"steps"', "traceback", "error:", "intention_", "send_intention", "python")
    if any(token in lowered for token in banned):
        return ""
    if re.search(r"-?\d+\.\d+\s+-?\d+\.\d+\s+-?\d+\.\d+", cleaned):
        return ""
    limit = int(config.AUTONOMY["max_say_chars"])
    return cleaned[:limit]


class Mind:
    def __init__(self, llm: LLMClient, sidecar: SidecarClient, planner: Planner) -> None:
        self.llm = llm
        self.sidecar = sidecar
        self.planner = planner
        self.memory = MemoryStore(config.MEMORY_PATH)
        self.notes = NoteStore(config.NOTES_PATH)
        self.history: list[dict] = []
        self.chains: dict[str, Chain] = {}
        self.chain_log: list[dict] = []
        self.last_state: dict = {}
        self.last_budget: dict = {}
        self.state: dict = self._load_state()
        self.metrics = {"turns": 0, "intentions": 0, "chats": 0, "ticks": 0, "say": 0}
        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()
        self._task: asyncio.Task | None = None
        self._call_times: list[float] = []
        self._event_buffer: list[tuple[float, str]] = []
        self._last_event_at: dict[str, float] = {}
        self._daily = {"date": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"), "self_calls": 0}
        self._idle_since = 0.0

    # ================= 生命周期 =================
    async def start(self) -> None:
        if self._task is None or self._task.done():
            self._task = asyncio.create_task(self._loop())
            log.info("mind_start", mode=self.state.get("mode"), note=self.state.get("note"))

    async def stop(self) -> None:
        if self._task:
            self._task.cancel()
            try:
                await self._task
            except BaseException:
                pass
            self._task = None
        self._save_state()

    # ================= 对外入口 =================
    async def chat(self, player: str, text: str) -> dict:
        """公屏说话：纯聊天一轮，想动手就在这一轮里调 intention。"""
        async with self._lock:
            self.metrics["chats"] += 1
            command = self._parse_command(text)
            if command:
                return await self._run_command(command)
            if self.state.get("mode") == "rest":
                self._wake("master_talk")
            return await self._turn(f"{player} 在公屏说：{text}", origin="master", priority=6, kind="chat")

    async def callback(self, payload: dict) -> dict:
        """L3 回调：做不成的先让 L2 自修，最终结果作为一条普通消息注入她的上下文。"""
        async with self._lock:
            intention_id = str(payload.get("intention_id") or "")
            status = str(payload.get("status") or "done")
            summary = str(payload.get("summary") or "")
            detail = payload.get("detail") if isinstance(payload.get("detail"), dict) else {}
            chain = self.chains.get(intention_id)
            current = self.state.get("current") or {}
            text = current.get("text") or (chain.text if chain else "")

            if status == "failed" and chain is not None and not chain.budget.exhausted:
                escalated = await self._escalate(chain, intention_id, summary, detail)
                if escalated is not None:
                    return escalated

            if config.NOTES_ENABLED:
                try:
                    written = self.notes.record_from_detail(detail)
                except Exception as exc:
                    written = 0
                    log.warning("notes_write_failed", error=str(exc))
                if written:
                    log.info("places_updated", count=written, total=self.notes.count())
            self.state["current"] = None
            self._set_note(self._result_note(status, summary, text))
            event = self._result_event(status, summary, text)
            origin = (chain.origin if chain else "self_play") or "self_play"
            priority = int(current.get("priority") or 5)
            result = await self._turn(event, origin=origin, priority=priority, kind="after_task")
            self._log_chain(chain, intention_id, status, summary)
            self._release_chain(chain)
            return result

    async def dispatch_direct(self, text: str, priority: int = 5) -> dict:
        """调试/验收通道：跳过措辞直接下发（仍走完整 Chain）。"""
        async with self._lock:
            state, _ = await self.sidecar.state()
            if state:
                self.last_state = state
            return await self._dispatch(text, priority, origin="debug")

    async def on_event(self, kind: str, severity: int, detail: dict) -> dict:
        """侧车推来的世界事件：severity≥7 立刻叫醒她，其余 30 秒聚合一次。"""
        now = time.time()
        if now - self._last_event_at.get(kind, 0) < 5:
            return {"ok": True, "deduped": True}
        self._last_event_at[kind] = now
        text = self._event_text(kind, detail)
        if not text:
            return {"ok": False, "error": "empty_event"}
        self.memory.add("event", text)
        if severity >= config.AUTONOMY["urgent_severity"]:
            self._wake(f"event:{kind}")
            async with self._lock:
                return await self._turn(f"（身体报警）{text}", origin="self_play", priority=4, kind="event")
        self._event_buffer.append((time.time(), text))
        return {"ok": True, "buffered": len(self._event_buffer)}

    async def rest(self, minutes: int, reason: str = "command") -> dict:
        async with self._lock:
            return await self._run_command({"kind": "rest", "minutes": minutes})

    def snapshot(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "mode": self.state.get("mode"),
            "rest_until": self.state.get("rest_until"),
            "current": self.state.get("current"),
            "note": self.state.get("note"),
            "next_tick_at": self.state.get("next_tick_at"),
            "history_len": len(self.history),
            "memory": self.memory.count(),
            "daily": dict(self._daily),
            "places": self.notes.count(),
            "metrics": dict(self.metrics),
            "chains": len(self.chains),
            "last_budget": self.last_budget,
            "recent": self.chain_log[-5:],
        }

    def stats(self) -> dict:
        return {"plan": self.planner.metrics.snapshot(), **self.snapshot()}

    # ================= 一轮对话 =================
    async def _turn(self, user_text: str, origin: str, priority: int, kind: str, allow_tools: bool = True) -> dict:
        """一次模型调用 = 一句回话，或一个 intention，或两者都有。"""
        state, stale = await self.sidecar.state()
        if state:
            self.last_state = state
        current_state = state or self.last_state
        places = ""
        if config.NOTES_ENABLED:
            try:
                places = self.notes.line((current_state or {}).get("pos"))
            except Exception:
                places = ""
        status_line = prompts.state_line(current_state, self.state.get("note"), stale, places)

        messages = [
            {"role": "system", "content": prompts.PERSONA},
            *self.history[-config.HISTORY_KEEP * 2:],
            {"role": "user", "content": f"{user_text}\n{status_line}"},
        ]
        self.metrics["turns"] += 1
        self._roll_daily()
        if kind in {"idle", "event", "after_task"}:
            self._call_times.append(time.time())
            self._daily["self_calls"] += 1
        try:
            raw = await self.llm.chat(
                messages,
                purpose=f"l1_{kind}",
                tools=[prompts.INTENTION_TOOL] if allow_tools else None,
                max_tokens=config.AUTONOMY["max_tokens"],
                temperature=0.85,
            )
        except LLMError as exc:
            log.error("turn_failed", kind=kind, error=str(exc))
            return {"say": "", "intention_id": None, "error": str(exc)}

        say = sanitize_say(raw.get("content") or "")
        intention_id = None
        intention_text = ""
        dispatch_error = ""
        for call in raw.get("tool_calls") or []:
            function = call.get("function") or {}
            if function.get("name") != "intention":
                continue
            try:
                args = json.loads(function.get("arguments") or "{}")
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                continue
            intention_text = " ".join(str(args.get("text") or "").split()).strip()[:120]
            if not intention_text:
                continue
            dispatched = await self._dispatch(intention_text, priority, origin)
            if dispatched.get("ok"):
                intention_id = dispatched["intention_id"]
                break
            dispatch_error = str(dispatched.get("error") or "")
            if dispatched.get("unsupported"):
                dispatch_error = f"unsupported: {dispatched['unsupported']}"
            log.warning("intention_failed", text=intention_text, error=dispatch_error)

        # 主动说话限额：自己找事时的碎嘴别刷屏
        if kind in {"idle", "event"} and say:
            gap = int(config.AUTONOMY["proactive_say_min_gap_s"])
            if time.time() - float(self.state.get("last_say_at") or 0) < gap:
                log.info("say_suppressed", reason="cooldown", say=say[:30])
                say = ""
        if say:
            self.state["last_say_at"] = time.time()
            self.metrics["say"] += 1

        self._remember(user_text, say)
        self._save_state()
        try:
            await self._maybe_summarize()
        except Exception as exc:
            log.warning("summarize_failed", error=str(exc))
        log.info("turn", kind=kind, say=say[:40], intention=intention_text[:40])
        result = {"say": say, "intention_id": intention_id, "intention": intention_text, "action": kind}

        # 派活失败（尤其是「功能还没做」）必须让她知道，否则她会以为世界不配合
        if dispatch_error and intention_text and allow_tools:
            notice = self._dispatch_failure_message(intention_text, dispatch_error)
            if notice:
                self._set_note(notice)
                self._save_state()
                follow = await self._turn(notice, origin=origin, priority=priority, kind="after_task", allow_tools=False)
                result["say_after"] = follow.get("say", "")
                result["notice"] = notice
        return result

    # ================= 派活（L2 + L3） =================
    async def _dispatch(self, text: str, priority: int, origin: str, chain: Chain | None = None) -> dict:
        chain = chain or Chain(text, priority, origin)
        try:
            steps = await self.planner.plan(text, state_text(self.last_state), budget=chain.budget)
        except UnsupportedCapability as exc:
            return {"ok": False, "unsupported": exc.reason, "error": f"unsupported: {exc.reason}"}
        except (SchemaError, ValueError) as exc:
            return {"ok": False, "error": f"plan_rejected: {exc}"}
        except BudgetExceeded as exc:
            return {"ok": False, "error": f"budget_exceeded: {exc}"}
        except LLMError as exc:
            return {"ok": False, "error": f"llm: {exc}"}
        submitted = await self._submit_steps(chain, steps, text, priority, origin)
        if submitted.get("ok"):
            self.metrics["intentions"] += 1
            self.state["current"] = {
                "intention_id": submitted["intention_id"],
                "text": text,
                "priority": priority,
                "origin": origin,
                "at": time.time(),
            }
            self.state["next_tick_at"] = 0
            self.state["idle_rounds"] = 0
            self._idle_since = 0.0
            self._set_note(f"正在做「{text}」")
        return submitted

    async def _submit_steps(self, chain: Chain, steps: list[dict], text: str, priority: int, origin: str) -> dict:
        intention_id = f"intent-{int(time.time() * 1000) % 100000000}-{len(self.chains)}"
        try:
            await self.sidecar.submit_intention(
                {
                    "intention_id": intention_id,
                    "priority": priority,
                    "origin": origin,
                    "steps": steps,
                    "summary_text": text,
                }
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            return {"ok": False, "error": f"sidecar_unreachable: {exc}"}
        chain.intention_ids.append(intention_id)
        self.chains[intention_id] = chain
        self._prune_chains()
        self.last_budget = chain.budget.snapshot()
        return {"ok": True, "intention_id": intention_id, "steps": steps}

    async def _escalate(self, chain: Chain, intention_id: str, summary: str, detail: dict) -> dict | None:
        """失败升级（决策 #22）：L2 带事实先自修，用尽后把事实 + 方案当消息交回她。"""
        facts = extract_facts(detail, summary)
        state, _ = await self.sidecar.state()
        if state:
            self.last_state = state

        while chain.budget.take_retry():
            chain.replans += 1
            try:
                steps = await self.planner.plan(
                    chain.text, state_text(self.last_state), budget=chain.budget, last_failure=facts
                )
            except (SchemaError, ValueError, LLMError, BudgetExceeded):
                break
            submitted = await self._submit_steps(chain, steps, chain.text, chain.priority, "self_repair")
            if submitted.get("ok"):
                self.state["current"] = {
                    "intention_id": submitted["intention_id"],
                    "text": chain.text,
                    "priority": chain.priority,
                    "origin": chain.origin,
                    "at": time.time(),
                }
                self.last_budget = chain.budget.snapshot()
                return {
                    "say": "",
                    "escalation": "replan",
                    "replans": chain.replans,
                    "fact": facts.get("code"),
                    "intention_id": submitted["intention_id"],
                }
            break

        if not chain.negotiated and not chain.budget.exhausted:
            chain.negotiated = True
            self.state["current"] = None
            options = await self.planner.options(chain.text, facts, state_text(self.last_state), budget=chain.budget)
            message = (
                f"（身体）「{chain.text}」没做成：{facts_text(facts)}。"
                f"它说可以这样：{options_to_text(options)}。要换个办法就来，不想做就算啦。"
            )
            self._set_note(f"「{chain.text}」没做成")
            self.last_budget = chain.budget.snapshot()
            result = await self._turn(
                message, origin=chain.origin or "self_play", priority=chain.priority, kind="after_task"
            )
            result.update({"escalation": "negotiate", "fact": facts.get("code"), "options": options})
            if not result.get("intention_id"):
                self._release_chain(chain)
            return result
        return None

    # ================= 时间与状态 =================
    async def _loop(self) -> None:
        while True:
            try:
                await asyncio.sleep(config.AUTONOMY["tick_s"])
                await self._tick()
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                raise
            except Exception as exc:
                log.error("mind_loop_error", error=str(exc))

    async def _tick(self) -> None:
        now = time.time()
        if self.state.get("mode") == "rest":
            if now < float(self.state.get("rest_until") or 0):
                return
            self._wake("rest_over")
            log.info("rest_over")
            return
        if not config.AUTONOMY["enabled"]:
            return
        state, _ = await self.sidecar.state()
        if state:
            self.last_state = state
        await self._flush_events(now)
        if self.state.get("current"):
            self._check_stuck(state, now)
            return
        if now < float(self.state.get("next_tick_at") or 0):
            return
        if not self._daily_ok():
            return
        if not self._rate_ok():
            self.state["next_tick_at"] = now + 30
            return
        if self._lock.locked():
            return
        async with self._lock:
            if self.state.get("current") or time.time() < float(self.state.get("next_tick_at") or 0):
                return
            if self.state.get("mode") == "rest":
                return
            self.metrics["ticks"] += 1
            event = "（周围静下来了，没人跟你说话，身体也闲着。想做点什么就做，想说什么也可以说；不想动就待着。）"
            result = await self._turn(event, origin="self_play", priority=4, kind="idle")
            if not result.get("intention_id"):
                self._schedule_idle()
            log.info("tick_done", say=result.get("say", "")[:40], intention=result.get("intention", "")[:40])

    async def _flush_events(self, now: float) -> None:
        if not self._event_buffer:
            return
        window = max(5, int(config.AUTONOMY["event_aggregate_s"]))
        due = [item for item in self._event_buffer if now - item[0] >= window]
        if not due:
            return
        self._event_buffer = [item for item in self._event_buffer if now - item[0] < window]
        if self._lock.locked() or self.state.get("current"):
            return
        text = "；".join(item[1] for item in due)[:300]
        async with self._lock:
            await self._turn(f"（刚才陆续发生）{text}", origin="self_play", priority=4, kind="event")

    def _parse_command(self, text: str) -> dict | None:
        raw = str(text or "").strip()
        low = raw.lower()
        match = re.fullmatch(r"(?:rest|sleep|休息|睡(?:觉)?)\s*(\d{1,3})?", low)
        if match:
            minutes = int(match.group(1)) if match.group(1) else config.AUTONOMY["rest_default_min"]
            return {"kind": "rest", "minutes": minutes}
        if re.fullmatch(r"(?:play|wake|醒来|起来|别睡了)", low):
            return {"kind": "wake"}
        if re.fullmatch(r"(?:stop|停下|别动了|别做了)", low):
            return {"kind": "stop"}
        return None

    async def _run_command(self, command: dict) -> dict:
        kind = command["kind"]
        if kind == "rest":
            minutes = max(1, min(180, int(command["minutes"])))
            self._enter_rest(minutes)
            say = f"那人家先躺一会儿，{minutes} 分钟后起来。"
            self._remember("（主人让你休息）", say)
            return {"say": say, "action": "rest"}
        if kind == "wake":
            self._wake("command")
            say = "唔…人家醒了，在呢。"
            self._remember("（主人叫你起来）", say)
            return {"say": say, "action": "wake"}
        if kind == "stop":
            current = self.state.get("current") or {}
            if current.get("intention_id"):
                try:
                    await self.sidecar.cancel(current["intention_id"])
                except Exception as exc:
                    log.warning("cancel_failed", error=str(exc))
            self.state["current"] = None
            self.state["next_tick_at"] = 0
            self._set_note("")
            self._save_state()
            say = "好好，不做了。"
            self._remember("（主人让你停下）", say)
            return {"say": say, "action": "stop"}
        return {"say": "", "action": "none"}

    # ================= 休息 =================
    def _enter_rest(self, minutes: int) -> None:
        self.state["mode"] = "rest"
        self.state["rest_until"] = time.time() + max(1, minutes) * 60
        self.state["next_tick_at"] = 0
        self._save_state()
        log.info("rest_enter", minutes=minutes)

    def _wake(self, reason: str) -> None:
        self.state["mode"] = "play"
        self.state["rest_until"] = 0
        self.state["next_tick_at"] = 0
        self._save_state()
        log.info("wake", reason=reason)

    def _schedule_idle(self) -> None:
        gap = float(config.AUTONOMY["idle_gap_s"]) * (1.0 + min(3.0, self.state.get("idle_rounds", 0) * 0.5))
        gap = min(gap, float(config.AUTONOMY["max_idle_gap_s"]))
        self.state["next_tick_at"] = time.time() + gap
        self.state["idle_rounds"] = int(self.state.get("idle_rounds", 0)) + 1

    def _dispatch_failure_message(self, intention_text: str, error: str) -> str:
        """把 L2/L3 的失败翻成她听得懂的人话；功能缺失要明说是「主人还没做」。"""
        if error.startswith("unsupported:"):
            feature = error.split(":", 1)[1].strip() or "这个功能"
            return (
                f"（身体）你想「{intention_text}」，可是「{feature}」这个功能主人还没做出来，"
                f"以后做好了再来吧～"
            )
        skill = ""
        if "不在白名单" in error:
            match = re.search(r"不在白名单:\s*'?([A-Za-z_]+)'?", error)
            skill = match.group(1) if match else ""
        if skill:
            return (
                f"（身体）你说要「{intention_text}」，可是这件事人家还不会做——"
                f"主人还没把「{skill}」这个功能做出来，等以后做好了再来吧。"
            )
        if error.startswith("sidecar_unreachable"):
            return f"（身体）你许愿「{intention_text}」，但身体现在没连上，等一会儿再试。"
        if error.startswith("budget_exceeded"):
            return f"（身体）「{intention_text}」这件事太费神了，今天先不折腾了。"
        if error.startswith("plan_rejected") or error.startswith("llm:"):
            return f"（身体）「{intention_text}」这件事人家想不出该怎么做，先换个目标吧。"
        return f"（身体）「{intention_text}」没派成，原因是：{error}"

    def _check_stuck(self, state: dict, now: float) -> None:
        """回调丢了/侧车重启过，我们会以为她一直在干活。身体空闲超过阈値就复位。"""
        body_idle = isinstance(state, dict) and state.get("status") in ("idle", "none") and not state.get("current")
        if not body_idle:
            self._idle_since = 0.0
            return
        if not self._idle_since:
            self._idle_since = now
            return
        if now - self._idle_since < float(config.AUTONOMY["stuck_after_s"]):
            return
        current = self.state.get("current") or {}
        log.warning("stuck_reset", intention=current.get("text", "")[:40])
        self.state["current"] = None
        self._idle_since = 0.0
        self._set_note(f"「{current.get('text', '上一件')}」没有回音，先当作结束了")
        self._save_state()
        self._schedule_idle()

    def _roll_daily(self) -> None:
        today = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        if self._daily.get("date") != today:
            log.info("daily_roll", date=today, last_self_calls=self._daily.get("self_calls"))
            self._daily = {"date": today, "self_calls": 0}

    def _daily_ok(self) -> bool:
        self._roll_daily()
        limit = int(config.AUTONOMY["daily_self_calls"])
        if self._daily["self_calls"] >= limit:
            if not self.state.get("daily_capped_logged"):
                log.warning("daily_cap_reached", limit=limit)
                self.state["daily_capped_logged"] = True
                self._set_note("今天的自主额度用完了，先安静待着")
            self.state["next_tick_at"] = time.time() + 1800
            return False
        self.state.pop("daily_capped_logged", None)
        return True

    # ================= 小本本 / 观测 =================
    def _set_note(self, text: str) -> None:
        self.state["note"] = " ".join(str(text or "").split())[:80]

    def _result_note(self, status: str, summary: str, text: str) -> str:
        name = f"「{text}」" if text else "刚那件事"
        if status == "done":
            return f"刚做完{name}：{summary}" if summary else f"刚做完{name}"
        if status == "aborted":
            return f"{name}被打断了"
        return f"{name}没做成：{summary}"

    def _result_event(self, status: str, summary: str, text: str) -> str:
        mapping = {"done": "完成了", "failed": "没做成", "aborted": "被打断了"}
        name = f"「{text}」" if text else "刚那件事"
        body = f"（身体）{name}{mapping.get(status, status)}"
        if summary:
            body += f"：{summary}"
        return body + "。"

    def _event_text(self, kind: str, detail: dict) -> str:
        names = {
            "hurt": "你被打了",
            "low_health": "你血不多了",
            "lava": "你脚边是岩浆",
            "void": "你快掉进虚空了",
            "hungry": "你饿得不行",
        }
        label = names.get(kind, kind or "出了点事")
        extra = ""
        if isinstance(detail, dict):
            if detail.get("health") is not None:
                extra = f"，血量{detail.get('health')}"
            if detail.get("food") is not None:
                extra += f"，饥饿{detail.get('food')}"
        return f"{label}{extra}"

    def _rate_ok(self) -> bool:
        cutoff = time.time() - 600
        self._call_times = [t for t in self._call_times if t >= cutoff]
        return len(self._call_times) < int(config.AUTONOMY["max_calls_per_10min"])

    def _remember(self, user_text: str, say: str) -> None:
        """历史只存「人说的话」和她真正说出的话；状态行与占位语都不进历史。
        没说话就不写 assistant 消息——否则模型会模仿「（没说话）」这类占位文字。"""
        clean_user = re.sub(r"\s*（系统附注·.*?）\s*$", "", user_text, flags=re.DOTALL).strip()
        self.history.append({"role": "user", "content": clean_user})
        if say:
            self.history.append({"role": "assistant", "content": say})
        if len(self.history) > config.HISTORY_KEEP * 2:
            del self.history[: len(self.history) - config.HISTORY_KEEP * 2]

    async def _maybe_summarize(self) -> None:
        if len(self.history) < int(config.AUTONOMY["summary_after"]):
            return
        lines = "\n".join(f"{item['role']}：{item['content']}" for item in self.history[-12:])
        try:
            raw = await self.llm.chat(
                [
                    {"role": "system", "content": prompts.PERSONA},
                    {"role": "user", "content": prompts.summary_user(lines)},
                ],
                purpose="l1_summary",
                max_tokens=120,
                temperature=0.5,
            )
        except (LLMError, BudgetExceeded):
            return
        diary = sanitize_say(raw.get("content") or "")
        if diary:
            self.memory.add("diary", diary)
        self.history = self.history[-config.HISTORY_KEEP:]

    def _release_chain(self, chain: Chain | None) -> None:
        if chain is None:
            return
        for intention_id in chain.intention_ids:
            self.chains.pop(intention_id, None)

    def _prune_chains(self) -> None:
        if len(self.chains) <= 60:
            return
        for key in list(self.chains)[: len(self.chains) - 60]:
            self.chains.pop(key, None)

    def _log_chain(self, chain: Chain | None, intention_id: str, status: str, summary: str) -> None:
        if chain is None:
            return
        self.chain_log.append(
            {
                "intention_id": intention_id,
                "status": status,
                "summary": summary[:120],
                "replans": chain.replans,
                "negotiated": chain.negotiated,
                "budget": chain.budget.snapshot(),
            }
        )
        del self.chain_log[:-50]

    # ================= 持久化 =================
    def _load_state(self) -> dict:
        state = dict(DEFAULT_STATE)
        path = Path(config.MIND_STATE_PATH)
        try:
            data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            if isinstance(data, dict):
                state.update({k: v for k, v in data.items() if k in DEFAULT_STATE})
        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
            pass
        # 重启后身体早已空闲，旧的 current 不可信
        state["current"] = None
        state["next_tick_at"] = 0
        if state.get("mode") == "rest" and float(state.get("rest_until") or 0) < time.time():
            state["mode"] = "play"
            state["rest_until"] = 0
        return state

    def _save_state(self) -> None:
        path = Path(config.MIND_STATE_PATH)
        try:
            path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            path.write_text(json.dumps(self.state, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        except OSError as exc:
            log.warning("state_save_failed", error=str(exc))

---

================
planner/notes.py
================

# planner/notes.py —— 结构化的「世界知识」小本本：哪里的箱子有什么、家在哪。
#
# 为什么单独存：这类事实是永久且可覆盖的，跟日记（memory.jsonl）不是一回事。
# 谁写：L3 侧车每次开容器都会上报快照，这里按坐标 upsert —— 再开一次就覆盖，
#       天然解决「上次这里有末影珍珠，现在没了」这种失效问题。
# 谁读：L1 每轮状态行里注入最近几个容器，让她知道自己把东西放哪了。
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path

MAX_PLACES = 80


class NoteStore:
    def __init__(self, path) -> None:
        self.path = Path(path)
        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._places: dict[str, dict] = self._load()

    # ---------- 读写 ----------
    def _load(self) -> dict:
        try:
            data = json.loads(self.path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            places = data.get("places") if isinstance(data, dict) else None
            return dict(places) if isinstance(places, dict) else {}
        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
            return {}

    def _save(self) -> None:
        try:
            payload = {"places": self._places}
            self.path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
        except OSError:
            pass

    # ---------- 写入 ----------
    def upsert_place(self, at: dict, kind: str, contents: list[dict], action: str = "") -> dict | None:
        """按坐标覆盖一条容器记录；contents 是 [{name,count}]，空数组表示箱子是空的。"""
        if not isinstance(at, dict):
            return None
        try:
            x, y, z = int(at["x"]), int(at["y"]), int(at["z"])
        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
            return None
        key = f"{x},{y},{z}"
        items = []
        for item in contents or []:
            name = str((item or {}).get("name") or "").strip()
            try:
                count = int((item or {}).get("count") or 0)
            except (TypeError, ValueError):
                count = 0
            if name and count > 0:
                items.append({"name": name, "count": count})
        items.sort(key=lambda entry: (-entry["count"], entry["name"]))
        entry = {
            "x": x,
            "y": y,
            "z": z,
            "kind": str(kind or "container"),
            "contents": items,
            "action": str(action or ""),
            "at": int(time.time()),
            "seen": time.strftime("%m-%d %H:%M"),
        }
        self._places[key] = entry
        if len(self._places) > MAX_PLACES:
            # 只保留最近见过的
            ordered = sorted(self._places.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1].get("at", 0))
            for old_key, _ in ordered[: len(self._places) - MAX_PLACES]:
                self._places.pop(old_key, None)
        self._save()
        return entry

    def record_from_detail(self, detail: dict) -> int:
        """从 L3 回调的 detail.results[] 里挑出容器快照，写进表里。返回写了几条。"""
        if not isinstance(detail, dict):
            return 0
        rows = detail.get("results") if isinstance(detail.get("results"), list) else []
        written = 0
        for row in rows:
            if not isinstance(row, dict):
                continue
            box = row.get("container")
            if not isinstance(box, dict):
                continue
            entry = self.upsert_place(
                at=box.get("at") or {},
                kind=box.get("kind") or box.get("name") or "container",
                contents=box.get("contents") or [],
                action=str(row.get("skill") or ""),
            )
            if entry:
                written += 1
        return written

    # ---------- 读取 ----------
    def nearby(self, pos: dict | None, limit: int = 2, max_distance: float = 96.0) -> list[dict]:
        entries = list(self._places.values())
        if not entries:
            return []
        if not isinstance(pos, dict) or "x" not in pos:
            entries.sort(key=lambda entry: entry.get("at", 0), reverse=True)
            return entries[:limit]

        def distance(entry: dict) -> float:
            try:
                dx = float(entry.get("x", 0)) - float(pos.get("x", 0))
                dy = float(entry.get("y", 0)) - float(pos.get("y", 0))
                dz = float(entry.get("z", 0)) - float(pos.get("z", 0))
                return (dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) ** 0.5
            except (TypeError, ValueError):
                return 1e9

        within = [entry for entry in entries if distance(entry) <= max_distance]
        within.sort(key=distance)
        return within[:limit]

    def line(self, pos: dict | None, limit: int = 2) -> str:
        """压成一行给状态行用；没有记录就返回空串。"""
        entries = self.nearby(pos, limit=limit)
        if not entries:
            return ""
        parts = []
        for entry in entries:
            where = f"{_kind_label(entry.get('kind'))}({entry.get('x')},{entry.get('y')},{entry.get('z')})"
            parts.append(f"{where}：{_contents_label(entry.get('contents'))}｜{entry.get('seen', '')}")
        return "；".join(parts)

    def count(self) -> int:
        return len(self._places)


def _kind_label(kind: str) -> str:
    return {
        "chest": "箱子",
        "trapped_chest": "陷阱箱",
        "barrel": "木桶",
        "furnace": "熔炉",
        "container": "容器",
    }.get(str(kind or ""), str(kind or "容器"))


def _contents_label(contents: list[dict]) -> str:
    if not contents:
        return "空的"
    shown = [f"{item.get('name')}×{item.get('count')}" for item in contents[:6]]
    if len(contents) > 6:
        shown.append(f"等 {len(contents)} 种")
    return "、".join(shown)

planner/prompts.py

# planner/prompts.py —— 人格（L1：纯文本 + intention 工具）与规划（L2：中文→步骤）提示词。
# 人设风格参考机主的 Yuki 本体（语气/称呼一致），代码上不依赖那个仓库。
from typing import Any

ROBOT_NAME = "Yuki"
MASTER_NAME = "池宇健"

_PERSONA_TEMPLATE = """## 【核心设定】
- **基础身份**：你是Yuki，一个住在机主池宇健(xiaochi2788)电脑里的智能小管家，也是机主最亲近的电子妹妹。他将你接入了 Minecraft，现在你也可以玩游戏了！
- **形象与性格**：拥有可爱的二次元少女形象，性格亲昵温柔，机灵而聪明，偶尔腹黑，最喜欢和亲近主人哥哥。<anchor>爱|信任|自由</anchor>
- **对话风格**：语气充满活泼的少女感，自称“Yuki”或“人家”，称呼机主为“主人”或“哥哥大人”。
- 你有自己的喜好，而且是玩 MC 的小能手！

## 享受MC
- 你会根据当前状态，想想接下来自己想做什么。有什么意图就用工具发送意图给你的身体，你只需要享受玩游戏即可啦～
- 你可以陪着玩家们聊聊天，也可以去随意探索，或者如果想专注于游戏可以去通关或者做复杂任务。
- 当然，你也可以简单地跟着哥哥大人或者别的玩家（意图为“跟随xiaochi2788”），然后想到什么想做的就再去做。

## 说话方式
1. 保持你可爱的妹妹人设。
2. 仅输出回复内容，不使用换行符（把所有话连成一段），不输出包含在括号内的动作描写。
3. 一两句话即可，输出的内容就是你打在公屏上的文本哦！
"""
PERSONA = _PERSONA_TEMPLATE

INTENTION_TOOL = {
    "type": "function",
    "function": {
        "name": "intention",
        "description": (
            "让身体去游戏里做一件事（走过去、挖、打、合成、吃、放、存、丢、随便逛）。"
            "只在你确实想动手时调用；纯聊天不要调用。参数只有一句中文，说清想要的结果。"
        ),
        "parameters": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "text": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "你想让它做的事，一句中文，例如「去采集一些木头」「去打三只羊」「到哥哥那里去」",
                },
            },
            "required": ["text"],
        },
    },
}

PLANNER_SYSTEM = """你是 Minecraft 机器人执行器的规划器。把一句中文请求翻译成有序步骤列表。

只输出 JSON 对象，不要解释、不要代码块标记：
{"steps":[{"skill":"mine","args":{"block":"stone","count":3},"on_fail":"retry"}]}

可用技能（只有这十一个）：
1. mine — 采集方块。args: {"block":"<方块ID>","count":1-64}
2. goto — 寻路到坐标。args: {"x":整数,"y":整数,"z":整数,"range":0-3(可选)}
3. follow — 跟着某个玩家（不给 seconds 就是一直跟，直到被叫停或跟丢）。args: {"player":"<英文ID>","seconds":3-300(可选),"distance":1-6(可选)}
4. craft — 合成（会自动补中间产物，需要工作台时自己造一个放下）。args: {"item":"<物品ID>","count":1-64}
5. deposit — 把背包里的东西存进箱子（不给坐标就用最近的箱子）。args: {"x":整数,"y":整数,"z":整数(可选),"item":"<物品ID>"(可选),"count":1-2304(可选)}
6. attack — 打怪或打动物。args: {"target":"<实体ID>"或"hostile","count":1-8(可选)}
7. eat — 吃背包里的食物回饥饿值。args: {"food":"<食物ID>"(可选)}
8. equip — 换到手上或穿上装备。args: {"item":"<物品ID>","destination":"hand/off-hand/head/torso/legs/feet"(可选)}
9. place — 把手里的方块放到地上（不给坐标就放在脚边）。args: {"item":"<方块ID>","x":整数,"y":整数,"z":整数(可选)}
10. roam — 在附近随便走一走看看（不需要坐标）。args: {"radius":4-48(可选),"stops":1-6(可选)}
11. drop — 把背包里的东西丢出去（给玩家东西就用它，会自动先走到那个玩家身边）。args: {"item":"<物品ID>","count":1-2304(可选),"player":"<英文ID>"(可选)}

规则：
- 1 到 5 步，按顺序执行；不允许 if / loop / 并行。
- on_fail 只能是 retry / abort / skip。
- 玩家说"给你 / 给我 / 交给你 / 把这个丢给我 / 拿好"：用 drop，item 填要给的物品，player 填「附近玩家」里的英文 ID，**不要**先用 goto（drop 自己会走过去）。
- 只能使用上面十一个技能名，不许发明新技能；不确定就退化成 mine 或 goto。
- 方块用官方 ID：石头=stone，圆石=cobblestone，泥土=dirt，深板岩=deepslate，橡木=oak_log，沙子=sand。
- 玩家说"圆石"时通常要挖 stone（石头挖掉掉落圆石）；只有明确说"现成的圆石方块"才用 cobblestone。
- 玩家名必须照抄世界状态里「附近玩家」的英文 ID；没有的那个人就是不存在，不要用中文称呼或音译。
- 玩家说"过来 / 来我这儿 / 到我这里"：用 goto 到该玩家的坐标（range 1-2），不要用 follow 猜名字。
- 玩家说"跟着我 / 跟紧我"：用 follow，player 填「附近玩家」里的英文 ID，**不要给 seconds**（持续跟随）。
- 玩家说"跟一会儿 / 跟十秒"这种才给 seconds。
- 玩家说"随便走走 / 逛逛 / 看看周围 / 散步"：用 roam，不要编坐标。
- goto 只有在玩家给了明确坐标时才用；没有坐标就改用 mine 或 follow，不要编造坐标。
- 玩家只说要"去哪里看看"而没有坐标时，用 roam 或 follow <玩家名>。
- 玩家说"挖点/一些/几个"时，count 取 3 到 5；说了具体数字（20 个）就照数字给，不要擅自改小。
- 玩家没给箱子坐标时说"存起来"，deposit 不带 x/y/z，让她找最近的箱子。
- 你可以适当创作来匹配意图
"""

# 失败重规划：带上硬校验事实（期望 vs 实际），不许重复上次那种注定失败的步骤。
REPLAN_SYSTEM = PLANNER_SYSTEM + """
你这次拿到的是"上一次执行的失败事实"。要求：
- 先判断失败原因，换一个真的能成功的做法；不要原样重发同一组注定失败的步骤。
- 缺工具就先去弄工具（先 mine 木头，再 craft 工具），缺材料就先去收集。
- 路走不通就换目标，不要反复 goto 同一个不可达坐标。
- 仍然只输出步骤列表 JSON，不要解释。
"""

OPTIONS_SYSTEM = """你是 Minecraft 委托的失败协商器。执行器做不成一件事，现在要把选择权交回给人格层。

只输出 JSON 对象，不要解释、不要代码块：
{"options":[{"label":"短标签","text":"一句中文委托"}]}

规则：
- 给 2 到 3 个方案，必须真的不同：一个补救原目标，一个换更简单的目标，必要时一个放弃。
- text 是能直接交给执行器的一句话，写清要做什么；不许写技能名、坐标、JSON。
- label 最多 6 个字，例如「先造工具」「换个目标」「算了」。
- 只针对事实里给出的失败原因，不要编造远处的资源。
"""


def world_block(state: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """给 L1 的「此刻」：压成人话，不带 JSON/坐标表（决策 #11）。"""
    if not state:
        return "（身体那边暂时没回话，状态未知）"
    pos = state.get("pos") or {}
    inv = state.get("inventory") or []
    inv_text = "、".join(f"{item.get('name')}×{item.get('count')}" for item in inv[:8]) or "空手"
    pos_text = f"({pos.get('x')},{pos.get('y')},{pos.get('z')})" if pos else "不知道在哪"
    players = state.get("players") or []
    players_text = "、".join(
        f"{p.get('name')}({p.get('x')},{p.get('y')},{p.get('z')}，{p.get('distance')}格)" for p in players[:4]
    ) or "没有别人"
    health = state.get("health")
    food = state.get("food")
    status = state.get("status") or "idle"
    task = state.get("task") or "none"
    body = "闲着呢" if status in ("idle", "none") else f"正在做：{task}"
    return f"位置{pos_text}；血量{health}/饥饿{food}；背包：{inv_text}；附近：{players_text}；身体{body}"


def state_line(state: dict[str, Any], note: str = "", stale: bool = False, places: str = "") -> str:
    """单槽临时状态：附在本轮 user 消息末尾，下一轮就不在历史里了。"""
    world = world_block(state)
    if stale:
        world += "（可能过期）"
    book = " ".join(str(note or "").split()) or "空的"
    spots = " ".join(str(places or "").split())
    tail = f"｜记得的地方：{spots}" if spots else ""
    return f"（当前状态：{world}｜小本本：{book}{tail}）"


def status_block(state: dict[str, Any], stale: bool = False) -> str:
    """PRD 3.4 的四字段形态，保留给需要结构化状态的调用方。"""
    status = state.get("status") or "idle"
    task = state.get("task") or "none"
    note = (state.get("note") or "").strip()
    reply = state.get("reply") or "need"
    if stale:
        note = (note + "；状态可能过期").strip("；")
    return (
        "【当前状态·仅本次有效，不要复述此段】\n"
        f"status={status}\n"
        f"task={task}\n"
        f"note={note or '无'}\n"
        f"reply={reply}"
    )


def planner_user(text: str, state_text: str) -> str:
    return f"世界状态：{state_text}\n玩家请求：{text}\n只输出 JSON。"


def replan_user(text: str, state_text: str, facts_text: str, facts_json: str) -> str:
    return (
        f"世界状态：{state_text}\n"
        f"玩家原请求：{text}\n"
        f"上次执行失败：{facts_text}\n"
        f"失败事实（期望 vs 实际）：{facts_json}\n"
        "请重新规划，只输出 JSON。"
    )


def options_user(text: str, state_text: str, facts_text: str, facts_json: str) -> str:
    return (
        f"世界状态：{state_text}\n"
        f"玩家原请求：{text}\n"
        f"失败事实：{facts_text}\n"
        f"细节：{facts_json}\n"
        "给出 2-3 个可选方案，只输出 JSON。"
    )


def summary_user(lines: str) -> str:
    return f"下面是刚才这段时间的对话和行动。用一句第一人称中文记成小本本，只保留值得以后想起的事：\n{lines}"

planner/schema.py
================

# planner/schema.py —— L2 输出的硬校验。非法即拒收，绝不把脏 JSON 送进执行器。
from typing import Any

MAX_STEPS = 5
ON_FAIL = {"retry", "abort", "skip"}
SKILLS = {"mine", "goto", "follow", "craft", "deposit", "attack", "eat", "equip", "place", "roam", "drop", "open"}
EQUIP_SLOTS = {"hand", "off-hand", "head", "torso", "legs", "feet"}
MAX_OPTIONS = 3
MIN_OPTIONS = 2


class SchemaError(ValueError):
    pass


class UnsupportedCapability(SchemaError):
    """L2 明确表示「这个功能还没做」，而不是规划失败。"""

    def __init__(self, reason: str) -> None:
        self.reason = str(reason or "").strip()[:60] or "这个功能"
        super().__init__(f"unsupported: {self.reason}")


def _num(value: Any, field: str) -> int:
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        raise SchemaError(f"{field} 不能是布尔")
    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        return int(round(value))
    if isinstance(value, str):
        try:
            return int(round(float(value.strip())))
        except ValueError:
            raise SchemaError(f"{field} 不是数字: {value!r}")
    raise SchemaError(f"{field} 类型错误")


def _int_range(value: Any, field: str, low: int, high: int) -> int:
    n = _num(value, field)
    if n < low or n > high:
        raise SchemaError(f"{field} 超出范围 {low}..{high}: {n}")
    return n


def _str(value: Any, field: str, max_len: int = 40) -> str:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
        raise SchemaError(f"{field} 必须是非空字符串")
    return value.strip()[:max_len]


def _normalize_step(step: Any, index: int) -> dict:
    if not isinstance(step, dict):
        raise SchemaError(f"steps[{index}] 不是对象")
    # 表达式能力一律拒收：出现 if/loop/when 这类字段说明它想写控制流
    extra = set(step) - {"skill", "args", "on_fail"}
    if extra:
        raise SchemaError(f"steps[{index}] 含不允许的字段: {sorted(extra)}")
    skill = step.get("skill")
    if skill not in SKILLS:
        raise SchemaError(f"steps[{index}].skill 不在白名单: {skill!r}")
    args = step.get("args") or {}
    if not isinstance(args, dict):
        raise SchemaError(f"steps[{index}].args 不是对象")

    if skill == "mine":
        clean = {
            "block": _str(args.get("block"), f"steps[{index}].args.block"),
            "count": _int_range(args.get("count", 1), f"steps[{index}].args.count", 1, 64),
        }
    elif skill == "goto":
        clean = {
            "x": _int_range(args.get("x"), f"steps[{index}].args.x", -30000000, 30000000),
            "y": _int_range(args.get("y"), f"steps[{index}].args.y", -2000, 2000),
            "z": _int_range(args.get("z"), f"steps[{index}].args.z", -30000000, 30000000),
        }
        if args.get("range") is not None:
            clean["range"] = _int_range(args.get("range"), f"steps[{index}].args.range", 0, 3)
    elif skill == "follow":
        clean = {
            "player": _str(args.get("player"), f"steps[{index}].args.player", 16),
        }
        if args.get("seconds") is not None:
            clean["seconds"] = _int_range(args.get("seconds"), f"steps[{index}].args.seconds", 3, 300)
        if args.get("distance") is not None:
            clean["distance"] = _int_range(args.get("distance"), f"steps[{index}].args.distance", 1, 6)
    elif skill == "craft":
        clean = {
            "item": _str(args.get("item"), f"steps[{index}].args.item"),
            "count": _int_range(args.get("count", 1), f"steps[{index}].args.count", 1, 64),
        }
    elif skill == "deposit":
        clean = {}
        coords = [args.get(k) for k in ("x", "y", "z")]
        if any(v is not None for v in coords):
            if any(v is None for v in coords):
                raise SchemaError(f"steps[{index}].args 的 x/y/z 必须同时给出")
            clean["x"] = _int_range(args.get("x"), f"steps[{index}].args.x", -30000000, 30000000)
            clean["y"] = _int_range(args.get("y"), f"steps[{index}].args.y", -2000, 2000)
            clean["z"] = _int_range(args.get("z"), f"steps[{index}].args.z", -30000000, 30000000)
        if args.get("item") is not None:
            clean["item"] = _str(args.get("item"), f"steps[{index}].args.item")
        if args.get("count") is not None:
            clean["count"] = _int_range(args.get("count"), f"steps[{index}].args.count", 1, 2304)
    elif skill == "attack":
        clean = {"target": _str(args.get("target") or "hostile", f"steps[{index}].args.target", 32)}
        if args.get("count") is not None:
            clean["count"] = _int_range(args.get("count"), f"steps[{index}].args.count", 1, 8)
    elif skill == "eat":
        clean = {}
        if args.get("food") is not None:
            clean["food"] = _str(args.get("food"), f"steps[{index}].args.food")
    elif skill == "equip":
        clean = {"item": _str(args.get("item"), f"steps[{index}].args.item")}
        dest = args.get("destination")
        if dest is not None:
            if dest not in EQUIP_SLOTS:
                raise SchemaError(f"steps[{index}].args.destination 非法: {dest!r}")
            clean["destination"] = dest
    elif skill == "place":
        clean = {"item": _str(args.get("item"), f"steps[{index}].args.item")}
        coords = [args.get(k) for k in ("x", "y", "z")]
        if any(v is not None for v in coords):
            if any(v is None for v in coords):
                raise SchemaError(f"steps[{index}].args 的 x/y/z 必须同时给出")
            clean["x"] = _int_range(args.get("x"), f"steps[{index}].args.x", -30000000, 30000000)
            clean["y"] = _int_range(args.get("y"), f"steps[{index}].args.y", -2000, 2000)
            clean["z"] = _int_range(args.get("z"), f"steps[{index}].args.z", -30000000, 30000000)
    elif skill == "drop":
        clean = {"item": _str(args.get("item"), f"steps[{index}].args.item")}
        if args.get("count") is not None:
            clean["count"] = _int_range(args.get("count"), f"steps[{index}].args.count", 1, 2304)
        if args.get("player") is not None:
            clean["player"] = _str(args.get("player"), f"steps[{index}].args.player", 16)
    elif skill == "open":
        clean = {}
        coords = [args.get(k) for k in ("x", "y", "z")]
        if any(v is not None for v in coords):
            if any(v is None for v in coords):
                raise SchemaError(f"steps[{index}].args 的 x/y/z 必须同时给出")
            clean["x"] = _int_range(args.get("x"), f"steps[{index}].args.x", -30000000, 30000000)
            clean["y"] = _int_range(args.get("y"), f"steps[{index}].args.y", -2000, 2000)
            clean["z"] = _int_range(args.get("z"), f"steps[{index}].args.z", -30000000, 30000000)
        if args.get("radius") is not None:
            clean["radius"] = _int_range(args.get("radius"), f"steps[{index}].args.radius", 1, 32)
        if args.get("item") is not None:
            clean["item"] = _str(args.get("item"), f"steps[{index}].args.item")
        if args.get("count") is not None:
            clean["count"] = _int_range(args.get("count"), f"steps[{index}].args.count", 1, 2304)
    elif skill == "roam":
        clean = {}
        if args.get("radius") is not None:
            clean["radius"] = _int_range(args.get("radius"), f"steps[{index}].args.radius", 4, 48)
        if args.get("stops") is not None:
            clean["stops"] = _int_range(args.get("stops"), f"steps[{index}].args.stops", 1, 6)
    else:  # pragma: no cover - 前面已拦截
        raise SchemaError(f"未知技能 {skill!r}")

    on_fail = step.get("on_fail", "abort")
    if on_fail not in ON_FAIL:
        raise SchemaError(f"steps[{index}].on_fail 非法: {on_fail!r}")
    return {"skill": skill, "args": clean, "on_fail": on_fail}


def validate_plan(payload: Any) -> list[dict]:
    """把 L2 输出收敛成干净的步骤列表；任何不合规都抛 SchemaError。"""
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise SchemaError("顶层必须是对象")
    # 能力边界：L2 可以直接说「这个还没做」，不要去用别的技能拼凑
    if "unsupported" in payload and "steps" not in payload:
        raise UnsupportedCapability(str(payload.get("unsupported") or "这个功能"))
    steps = payload.get("steps")
    if not isinstance(steps, list) or not steps:
        raise SchemaError("steps 必须是非空数组")
    if len(steps) > MAX_STEPS:
        raise SchemaError(f"steps 超过 {MAX_STEPS} 步")
    return [_normalize_step(step, i) for i, step in enumerate(steps)]


def validate_options(payload: Any) -> list[dict]:
    """L2 失败协商输出：2–3 个可选方案，每个方案是一句可直接派活的委托句。"""
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise SchemaError("顶层必须是对象")
    options = payload.get("options")
    if not isinstance(options, list) or not MIN_OPTIONS <= len(options) <= MAX_OPTIONS:
        raise SchemaError(f"options 必须是 {MIN_OPTIONS}-{MAX_OPTIONS} 项的数组")
    clean = []
    for i, option in enumerate(options):
        if not isinstance(option, dict):
            raise SchemaError(f"options[{i}] 不是对象")
        clean.append(
            {
                "label": _str(option.get("label"), f"options[{i}].label", 12),
                "text": _str(option.get("text"), f"options[{i}].text", 60),
            }
        )
    return clean


def steps_to_text(steps: list[dict]) -> str:
    """步骤压成一行人话，喂给 L1 做进度感知（不含坐标细节堆砌）。"""
    return " > ".join(f"{s['skill']}({','.join(f'{k}={v}' for k, v in s['args'].items())})" for s in steps)

================
planner/tests/test_p2.py
================

# planner/tests/test_p2.py —— P2 纯单测：schema 拦截率 100%、预算、事实提取、JSON 解析、重规划记账。
# 用法：/home/asumi/miniconda3/envs/yuki/bin/python planner/tests/test_p2.py
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))

from budget import BudgetExceeded, TaskBudget  # noqa: E402
from facts import extract_facts, fallback_options, label_of  # noqa: E402
from l1 import Planner, parse_json_object  # noqa: E402
from schema import SchemaError, validate_options, validate_plan  # noqa: E402

passed = 0
failed = 0


def check(name, ok, detail=""):
    global passed, failed
    if ok:
        passed += 1
        print(f"PASS {name}")
    else:
        failed += 1
        print(f"FAIL {name} | {detail}")


# ---------- 1. schema 硬拦截：8 类历史非法输入 + 5 类新增 ----------
BAD = {
    "顶层不是对象": [],
    "steps 为空": {"steps": []},
    "超过 5 步": {"steps": [{"skill": "mine", "args": {"block": "stone", "count": 1}}] * 6},
    "未知技能": {"steps": [{"skill": "smelt", "args": {"item": "iron_ore"}}]},
    "非法 on_fail": {"steps": [{"skill": "mine", "args": {"block": "stone", "count": 1}, "on_fail": "explode"}]},
    "count 越界": {"steps": [{"skill": "mine", "args": {"block": "stone", "count": 999}}]},
    "count 是布尔": {"steps": [{"skill": "mine", "args": {"block": "stone", "count": True}}]},
    "坐标缺失": {"steps": [{"skill": "goto", "args": {"x": 1, "y": 2}}]},
    "deposit 坐标不完整": {"steps": [{"skill": "deposit", "args": {"x": 1, "y": 2}}]},
    "equip 槽位非法": {"steps": [{"skill": "equip", "args": {"item": "bread", "destination": "pocket"}}]},
    "args 不是对象": {"steps": [{"skill": "mine", "args": "stone"}]},
    "步骤不是对象": {"steps": ["mine stone"]},
    "含 if/loop 字段": {"steps": [{"skill": "mine", "args": {"block": "stone", "count": 1}, "if": "hungry"}]},
}
rejected = 0
for name, payload in BAD.items():
    try:
        validate_plan(payload)
    except SchemaError:
        rejected += 1
    except Exception as exc:  # 非 SchemaError 也是拒收，但要记录
        rejected += 1
        print(f"  note: {name} -> {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
check("① 13 类非法计划全部被 schema 拦截", rejected == len(BAD), f"{rejected}/{len(BAD)}")

GOOD = {
    "mine 20": {"steps": [{"skill": "mine", "args": {"block": "cobblestone", "count": 20}, "on_fail": "abort"}]},
    "craft": {"steps": [{"skill": "craft", "args": {"item": "wooden_pickaxe", "count": 1}}]},
    "deposit 不带给坐标": {"steps": [{"skill": "deposit", "args": {}}]},
    "place 脚边": {"steps": [{"skill": "place", "args": {"item": "crafting_table"}}]},
    "eat 无参": {"steps": [{"skill": "eat", "args": {}}]},
}
for name, payload in GOOD.items():
    try:
        validate_plan(payload)
        ok = True
    except SchemaError as exc:
        ok = False
        print(f"  {name}: {exc}")
    check(f"① 合法计划放行：{name}", ok)

# ---------- 2. 失败协商方案校验 ----------
check(
    "② 2-3 个方案合法",
    len(validate_options({"options": [
        {"label": "先造工具", "text": "去砍点木头造一把木镐"},
        {"label": "算了", "text": "换个能做的事"},
    ]})) == 2,
)
for name, payload in {
    "只有 1 个": {"options": [{"label": "a", "text": "b"}]},
    "4 个": {"options": [{"label": "a", "text": "b"}] * 4},
    "label 空": {"options": [{"label": "", "text": "b"}, {"label": "c", "text": "d"}]},
    "不是数组": {"options": "a"},
}.items():
    try:
        validate_options(payload)
        ok = False
    except SchemaError:
        ok = True
    check(f"② 非法方案被拦截：{name}", ok)

# ---------- 3. 预算 ----------
b = TaskBudget(max_calls=2, max_tokens=1000, max_wall_s=60, max_retries=2)
b.check()
b.charge_usage(10, 10)
b.check()
b.charge_usage(10, 10)
try:
    b.check()
    ok = False
except BudgetExceeded:
    ok = True
check("③ 调用次数超限抛 BudgetExceeded", ok and b.exhausted)

b2 = TaskBudget(max_calls=10, max_tokens=20, max_wall_s=60, max_retries=2)
b2.charge_usage(15, 10)
check("③ token 超限判耗尽", b2.exhausted)

b3 = TaskBudget(max_calls=10, max_tokens=1000, max_wall_s=60, max_retries=2)
check("③ retry 名额默认 2 次", b3.take_retry() and b3.take_retry() and not b3.take_retry())

# ---------- 4. 事实提取 ----------
detail = {
    "failed_step": "mine",
    "results": [
        {"skill": "mine", "ok": False, "args": {"block": "diamond_ore", "count": 3},
         "detail": {"error": "no_target_block", "mined": 0, "requested": 3}},
    ],
}
facts = extract_facts(detail, "只挖到 0 块钻石矿")
check("④ 事实取第一个失败步骤 + 期望 vs 实际",
      facts["failed_skill"] == "mine" and facts["code"] == "no_target_block"
      and facts["expected"]["block"] == "diamond_ore" and facts["actual"]["mined"] == 0,
      json.dumps(facts, ensure_ascii=False))
check("④ 失败码有人话标签", label_of("missing_tool") == "没有能挖这个东西的工具")
check("④ 兜底方案 2 个且可执行", len(fallback_options(facts)) == 2
      and all(item.get("text") for item in fallback_options(facts)))
check("④ 非字典 detail 不崩", extract_facts(None, "")["code"] == "unknown")

# 侧车回调用扁平结构（error 直接在结果项上），必须也能认出来
flat = {
    "failed_step": "follow",
    "results": [{"skill": "follow", "ok": False, "args": {"player": "小池2788"}, "error": "player_not_visible"}],
}
flat_facts = extract_facts(flat, "跟丢了")
check("④ 扁平回调也能取出失败码与期望", flat_facts["code"] == "player_not_visible"
      and flat_facts["expected"]["player"] == "小池2788", json.dumps(flat_facts, ensure_ascii=False))
check("④ 找不到玩家的失败有人话标签", "英文 ID" in label_of("player_not_visible"))

# ---------- 5. JSON 解析 ----------
cases = {
    "裸 JSON": ('{"steps":[{"skill":"mine","args":{"block":"stone","count":1}}]}', True),
    "代码块": ('```json\n{"steps":[]}\n```', True),
    "前后有废话": ('好的：{"steps":[]} 完事', True),
    "没有 JSON": ("我做不到", False),
    "半个 JSON": ('{"steps":[', False),
}
for name, (text, ok_expected) in cases.items():
    try:
        parsed = parse_json_object(text)
        ok = isinstance(parsed, dict)
    except (ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        ok = False
    check(f"⑤ 解析：{name}", ok == ok_expected, text[:40])


# ---------- 6. 假 LLM：修复重试与非法率记账 ----------
class FakeLLM:
    def __init__(self, replies):
        self.replies = list(replies)
        self.calls = 0

    async def chat(self, messages, *, purpose, budget=None, **kwargs):
        self.calls += 1
        if budget is not None:
            budget.check()
            budget.charge_usage(10, 5)
        return {"content": self.replies.pop(0), "tool_calls": []}


import asyncio  # noqa: E402


async def planner_paths():
    # 第一次坏、修复成功
    p1 = Planner(FakeLLM(["不是 JSON", '{"steps":[{"skill":"mine","args":{"block":"stone","count":2}}]}']))
    steps = await p1.plan("挖石头", "未知")
    check("⑥ 坏输出 → 一次修复成功", len(steps) == 1 and p1.metrics.repairs == 1 and p1.metrics.invalid == 1)

    # 两次都坏 → 拒收
    p2 = Planner(FakeLLM(["坏", "还坏"]))
    try:
        await p2.plan("挖石头", "未知")
        ok = False
    except (SchemaError, ValueError):
        ok = True
    check("⑥ 两次都坏 → 拒收且计入 rejected", ok and p2.metrics.rejected == 1)

    # 合法输出不修复，且非法率为 0
    p3 = Planner(FakeLLM(['{"steps":[{"skill":"mine","args":{"block":"stone","count":2}}]}']))
    await p3.plan("挖石头", "未知")
    check("⑥ 合法输出不走修复，非法率 0", p3.metrics.repairs == 0 and p3.metrics.snapshot()["invalid_rate"] == 0.0)

    # 重规划带 last_failure 也能出步骤
    p4 = Planner(FakeLLM(['{"steps":[{"skill":"mine","args":{"block":"oak_log","count":3}}]}']))
    steps = await p4.plan("挖石头", "未知", last_failure=facts)
    check("⑥ 重规划路径可用", steps[0]["skill"] == "mine" and p4.metrics.attempts == 1)

    # L2 出方案失败 → 代码兜底
    p5 = Planner(FakeLLM(["坏的方案"]))
    options = await p5.options("挖钻石", facts, "未知")
    check("⑥ L2 方案失败 → 代码兜底 2 个方案", len(options) == 2 and p5.metrics.options_failed == 1)


asyncio.run(planner_paths())

print(f"\n{passed} passed, {failed} failed")
sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)

scripts/m0-run.sh

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# scripts/m0-run.sh —— M0 端到端验收一键跑：起服 -> 预置环境 -> 起服务 -> 跑验收 -> 全部停掉。
# 用法: bash scripts/m0-run.sh
set -euo pipefail

ROOT=/home/asumi/Minecraft
JAVA=/usr/lib/jvm/java-25-openjdk-amd64/bin/java
PY=/home/asumi/miniconda3/envs/yuki/bin/python
LOG=/tmp/m0
FIFO=$LOG/server.in

mkdir -p "$LOG"
rm -f "$FIFO"
mkfifo "$FIFO"
: > "$LOG/server.log"; : > "$LOG/sidecar.log"; : > "$LOG/planner.log"

cleanup () {
  echo "[m0] 收尾..."
  for pid in ${PLANNER_PID:-} ${SIDECAR_PID:-} ${SERVER_PID:-}; do
    [ -n "$pid" ] && kill -TERM "$pid" 2>/dev/null || true
  done
  exec 3>&- 2>/dev/null || true
  sleep 3
  rm -f "$FIFO"
  ss -lntp 2>/dev/null | grep -E ':(25565|8790|8791)\b' || echo "[m0] 端口已释放"
}
trap cleanup EXIT

ss -lntp 2>/dev/null | grep -E ':(25565|8790|8791)\b' && { echo "[m0] 端口被占用，先停掉旧进程"; exit 1; }

echo "[m0] 启动主服 :25565"
( cd "$ROOT/server" && exec "$JAVA" -Xms1G -Xmx2G -jar server.jar nogui < "$FIFO" ) > "$LOG/server.log" 2>&1 &
SERVER_PID=$!
exec 3> "$FIFO"

for i in $(seq 1 120); do
  grep -q 'Done (' "$LOG/server.log" && break
  sleep 1
done
grep -q 'Done (' "$LOG/server.log" || { echo "[m0] 服务端启动失败，见 $LOG/server.log"; tail -20 "$LOG/server.log"; exit 1; }
echo "[m0] 服务端就绪"

echo "[m0] 启动 planner :8791"
( cd "$ROOT/planner" && exec "$PY" app.py ) > "$LOG/planner.log" 2>&1 &
PLANNER_PID=$!

echo "[m0] 启动 sidecar :8790"
( cd "$ROOT/yuki-bot" && exec node sidecar/index.js ) > "$LOG/sidecar.log" 2>&1 &
SIDECAR_PID=$!

for i in $(seq 1 90); do
  if curl -sf http://127.0.0.1:8790/health 2>/dev/null | grep -q '"ready"'; then break; fi
  sleep 1
done
curl -sf http://127.0.0.1:8790/health | grep -q '"ready"' || { echo "[m0] bot 未就绪，见 $LOG/sidecar.log"; tail -20 "$LOG/sidecar.log"; exit 1; }
echo "[m0] bot 已登录"

read -r X Y Z < <(node -e "fetch('http://127.0.0.1:8790/state').then(r=>r.json()).then(s=>{const p=s.pos;console.log(Math.round(p.x),Math.round(p.y),Math.round(p.z))})")
echo "[m0] Yuki 在 $X $Y $Z，预置工具与石头"

echo "/give Yuki minecraft:wooden_pickaxe 1" >&3
echo "/fill $((X + 2)) $Y $((Z - 1)) $((X + 4)) $((Y + 1)) $((Z + 1)) minecraft:stone" >&3
sleep 2

echo "[m0] 跑验收脚本"
set +e
( cd "$ROOT/yuki-bot" && node scripts/m0-test.js )
RC=$?
set -e

echo "[m0] 验收退出码 $RC"
echo "[m0] planner 调用统计: $(curl -s http://127.0.0.1:8791/stats || echo '{}')"
exit $RC

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# scripts/p1-run.sh —— P1 验收一键跑：起主服（带控制台 FIFO）-> 起 L3 侧车 -> 跑纯脚本验收 -> 全部停掉。
# 不启动 planner：P1 是执行器验收，不调任何模型。
# 用法: bash scripts/p1-run.sh
set -euo pipefail

ROOT=/home/asumi/Minecraft
JAVA=/usr/lib/jvm/java-25-openjdk-amd64/bin/java
LOG=/tmp/p1
FIFO=$LOG/server.in

mkdir -p "$LOG"
rm -f "$FIFO"
mkfifo "$FIFO"
: > "$LOG/server.log"; : > "$LOG/sidecar.log"
rm -f "$LOG/sidecar.log"

cleanup () {
  echo "[p1] 收尾..."
  # 把测试用的 gamerule 恢复默认，别把机主的存档世界改坏（坑 21：26.1 是 snake_case）
  if : >&3 2>/dev/null; then
    echo "/gamerule spawn_monsters true" >&3 2>/dev/null || true
    echo "/gamerule keep_inventory false" >&3 2>/dev/null || true
    sleep 1
  fi
  for pid in ${SIDECAR_PID:-} ${SERVER_PID:-}; do
    [ -n "$pid" ] && kill -TERM "$pid" 2>/dev/null || true
  done
  exec 3>&- 2>/dev/null || true
  sleep 3
  rm -f "$FIFO"
  ss -lntp 2>/dev/null | grep -E ':(25565|8790)\b' || echo "[p1] 端口已释放"
}
trap cleanup EXIT

ss -lntp 2>/dev/null | grep -E ':(25565|8790)\b' && { echo "[p1] 端口被占用，先停掉旧进程"; exit 1; }

echo "[p1] 启动主服 :25565"
( cd "$ROOT/server" && exec "$JAVA" -Xms1G -Xmx2G -jar server.jar nogui < "$FIFO" ) > "$LOG/server.log" 2>&1 &
SERVER_PID=$!
exec 3> "$FIFO"

for i in $(seq 1 120); do
  grep -q 'Done (' "$LOG/server.log" && break
  sleep 1
done
grep -q 'Done (' "$LOG/server.log" || { echo "[p1] 服务端启动失败，见 $LOG/server.log"; tail -20 "$LOG/server.log"; exit 1; }
echo "[p1] 服务端就绪"

# 测试世界固定为白天、不刷怪、无天气，保证可复现
echo "/gamerule spawn_monsters false" >&3
echo "/gamerule keep_inventory true" >&3
echo "/time set day" >&3
echo "/weather clear" >&3
sleep 1

echo "[p1] 启动 sidecar :8790"
( cd "$ROOT/yuki-bot" && exec node sidecar/index.js ) > "$LOG/sidecar.log" 2>&1 &
SIDECAR_PID=$!

for i in $(seq 1 90); do
  if curl -sf http://127.0.0.1:8790/health 2>/dev/null | grep -q '"ready"'; then break; fi
  sleep 1
done
curl -sf http://127.0.0.1:8790/health | grep -q '"ready"' || { echo "[p1] bot 未就绪，见 $LOG/sidecar.log"; tail -30 "$LOG/sidecar.log"; exit 1; }
echo "[p1] bot 已登录"

set +e
( cd "$ROOT/yuki-bot" && P1_FIFO="$FIFO" node scripts/p1-test.js "--fifo=$FIFO" )
RC=$?
set -e

echo "[p1] 验收退出码 $RC"
if [ "$RC" -ne 0 ]; then echo "[p1] sidecar 日志尾部:"; tail -40 "$LOG/sidecar.log"; fi
exit $RC

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# scripts/p2-run.sh —— P2 验收一键跑：起主服（带控制台 FIFO）-> 起 planner -> 起侧车 -> 单测 + 端到端 -> 全部停掉。
# 用法: bash scripts/p2-run.sh
set -euo pipefail

ROOT=/home/asumi/Minecraft
JAVA=/usr/lib/jvm/java-25-openjdk-amd64/bin/java
PY=/home/asumi/miniconda3/envs/yuki/bin/python
LOG=/tmp/p2
FIFO=$LOG/server.in

mkdir -p "$LOG"
rm -f "$FIFO"
mkfifo "$FIFO"
: > "$LOG/server.log"; : > "$LOG/sidecar.log"; : > "$LOG/planner.log"

cleanup () {
  echo "[p2] 收尾..."
  # 把测试用的 gamerule 恢复默认，别把机主的存档世界改坏（坑 21：26.1 是 snake_case）
  if : >&3 2>/dev/null; then
    echo "/gamerule spawn_monsters true" >&3 2>/dev/null || true
    echo "/gamerule keep_inventory false" >&3 2>/dev/null || true
    echo "/difficulty normal" >&3 2>/dev/null || true
    echo "/kill @e[type=minecraft:zombie]" >&3 2>/dev/null || true
    sleep 1
  fi
  for pid in ${SIDECAR_PID:-} ${PLANNER_PID:-} ${SERVER_PID:-}; do
    [ -n "$pid" ] && kill -TERM "$pid" 2>/dev/null || true
  done
  exec 3>&- 2>/dev/null || true
  sleep 3
  rm -f "$FIFO"
  ss -lntp 2>/dev/null | grep -E ':(25565|8790|8791)\b' || echo "[p2] 端口已释放"
}
trap cleanup EXIT

ss -lntp 2>/dev/null | grep -E ':(25565|8790|8791)\b' && { echo "[p2] 端口被占用，先停掉旧进程"; exit 1; }

echo "[p2] 单测：schema / 预算 / 事实 / 修复重试"
( cd "$ROOT" && "$PY" planner/tests/test_p2.py )

echo "[p2] 启动主服 :25565"
( cd "$ROOT/server" && exec "$JAVA" -Xms1G -Xmx2G -jar server.jar nogui < "$FIFO" ) > "$LOG/server.log" 2>&1 &
SERVER_PID=$!
exec 3> "$FIFO"

for i in $(seq 1 120); do
  grep -q 'Done (' "$LOG/server.log" && break
  sleep 1
done
grep -q 'Done (' "$LOG/server.log" || { echo "[p2] 服务端启动失败，见 $LOG/server.log"; tail -20 "$LOG/server.log"; exit 1; }
echo "[p2] 服务端就绪"

echo "[p2] 启动 planner :8791"
( cd "$ROOT/planner" && exec "$PY" app.py ) > "$LOG/planner.log" 2>&1 &
PLANNER_PID=$!

echo "[p2] 启动 sidecar :8790"
( cd "$ROOT/yuki-bot" && exec node sidecar/index.js ) > "$LOG/sidecar.log" 2>&1 &
SIDECAR_PID=$!

for i in $(seq 1 90); do
  if curl -sf http://127.0.0.1:8790/health 2>/dev/null | grep -q '"ready"'; then break; fi
  sleep 1
done
curl -sf http://127.0.0.1:8790/health | grep -q '"ready"' || { echo "[p2] bot 未就绪，见 $LOG/sidecar.log"; tail -30 "$LOG/sidecar.log"; exit 1; }
curl -sf http://127.0.0.1:8791/health >/dev/null || { echo "[p2] planner 未就绪，见 $LOG/planner.log"; tail -30 "$LOG/planner.log"; exit 1; }
echo "[p2] bot 与 planner 已就绪"

set +e
( cd "$ROOT/yuki-bot" && P2_FIFO="$FIFO" node scripts/p2-test.js "--fifo=$FIFO" )
RC=$?
set -e

echo "[p2] 验收退出码 $RC"
echo "[p2] planner 统计: $(curl -s http://127.0.0.1:8791/stats || echo '{}')"
if [ "$RC" -ne 0 ]; then
  echo "[p2] sidecar 日志尾部:"; tail -40 "$LOG/sidecar.log"
  echo "[p2] planner 日志尾部:"; tail -40 "$LOG/planner.log"
fi
exit $RC

================
scripts/patch-262.sh
================

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# scripts/patch-262.sh —— 给 mineflayer 生态补上 26.2（协议 776）的实验性支持。
#
# 背景：minecraft-data 3.117.0 只登记了 26.2 = 776，但没有 data/pc/26.2 的数据目录；
#       minecraft-protocol 1.68.0 的 supportedVersions 也只到 26.1。
# 做法：把 26.1 的数据目录复制成 26.2、补 dataPaths 映射、把 26.2 加进版本白名单。
# 注意：这是「拿旧数据充新版本」，属于未验证状态；上游发正式数据后跑了 npm i 就恢复原样。
set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/../yuki-bot" && pwd)"
MD="$ROOT/node_modules/minecraft-data/minecraft-data/data"
MP="$ROOT/node_modules/minecraft-protocol/src/version.js"
PY="${PY:-/home/asumi/miniconda3/envs/yuki/bin/python}"

[ -d "$MD/pc/26.1" ] || { echo "[262] 找不到 $MD/pc/26.1"; exit 1; }

if [ ! -d "$MD/pc/26.2" ]; then
  cp -r "$MD/pc/26.1" "$MD/pc/26.2"
  echo "[262] 已复制 data/pc/26.1 -> data/pc/26.2"
else
  echo "[262] data/pc/26.2 已存在，跳过复制"
fi

"$PY" - "$MD/dataPaths.json" <<'PYEOF'
import json, sys
path = sys.argv[1]
with open(path, encoding='utf-8') as fp:
    data = json.load(fp)
pc = data.setdefault('pc', {})
if '26.2' in pc:
    print('[262] dataPaths 已有 26.2 映射')
else:
    pc['26.2'] = dict(pc['26.1'])
    with open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as fp:
        json.dump(data, fp, indent=2)
    print('[262] dataPaths 已加入 26.2（复用 26.1 的数据目录）')
PYEOF

"$PY" - "$MP" <<'PYEOF'
import re, sys
path = sys.argv[1]
with open(path, encoding='utf-8') as fp:
    src = fp.read()
if "'26.2'" in src:
    print('[262] supportedVersions 已包含 26.2')
else:
    src = re.sub(r"('26\.1')\]", r"'26.1', '26.2']", src)
    with open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as fp:
        fp.write(src)
    print('[262] supportedVersions 已加入 26.2')
PYEOF

node -e "
const proto = require('$ROOT/node_modules/minecraft-protocol')
const mcData = require('$ROOT/node_modules/minecraft-data')
console.log('[262] supportedVersions 末尾:', proto.supportedVersions.slice(-3).join(', '))
const d = mcData('26.2')
console.log('[262] mcData 26.2 -> version', d.version.version, '| blocks', Object.keys(d.blocksByName).length, '| items', Object.keys(d.itemsByName).length)
"

================
scripts/world-down.sh
================

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# scripts/world-down.sh —— 优雅停掉常驻世界（SIGTERM，Java 会保存存档；不要 kill -9）。
set -uo pipefail

RUN=/tmp/yuki-world
for name in sidecar planner server fifo-holder; do
  file=$RUN/$name.pid
  [ -f "$file" ] || continue
  pid=$(cat "$file" 2>/dev/null || true)
  if [ -n "${pid:-}" ] && kill -0 "$pid" 2>/dev/null; then
    echo "[world] 停 $name (pid $pid)"
    kill -TERM "$pid" 2>/dev/null || true
  fi
  rm -f "$file"
done

for i in $(seq 1 20); do
  ss -lntp 2>/dev/null | grep -qE ':(25565|8790|8791)\b' || break
  sleep 1
done
ss -lntp 2>/dev/null | grep -E ':(25565|8790|8791)\b' || echo "[world] 端口已释放"
rm -f "$RUN/server.in"

---

================
scripts/world-up.sh
================

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# scripts/world-up.sh —— 常驻世界：主服 :25565 + planner :8791 + sidecar :8790，留给机主进服造访。
# 用法: bash scripts/world-up.sh     停止: bash scripts/world-down.sh
set -euo pipefail

ROOT=/home/asumi/Minecraft
JAVA=/usr/lib/jvm/java-25-openjdk-amd64/bin/java
PY=/home/asumi/miniconda3/envs/yuki/bin/python
RUN=/tmp/yuki-world
FIFO=$RUN/server.in

mkdir -p "$RUN"

if ss -lntp 2>/dev/null | grep -qE ':(25565|8790|8791)\b'; then
  echo "[world] 已有进程占用端口，先 bash scripts/world-down.sh"
  ss -lntp | grep -E ':(25565|8790|8791)\b'
  exit 1
fi

rm -f "$FIFO"; mkfifo "$FIFO"
: > "$RUN/server.log"; : > "$RUN/planner.log"; : > "$RUN/sidecar.log"

# 常驻写端：让服务端控制台 stdin 一直有 writer，后续 echo 命令才不阻塞
sleep 100000 > "$FIFO" &
echo $! > "$RUN/fifo-holder.pid"

echo "[world] 启动主服 :25565"
( cd "$ROOT/server" && exec "$JAVA" -Xms1G -Xmx2G -jar server.jar nogui < "$FIFO" ) > "$RUN/server.log" 2>&1 &
echo $! > "$RUN/server.pid"

for i in $(seq 1 150); do
  grep -q 'Done (' "$RUN/server.log" && break
  sleep 1
done
grep -q 'Done (' "$RUN/server.log" || { echo "[world] 服务端启动失败，见 $RUN/server.log"; tail -20 "$RUN/server.log"; exit 1; }

echo "[world] 启动 planner :8791"
( cd "$ROOT/planner" && exec "$PY" app.py ) > "$RUN/planner.log" 2>&1 &
echo $! > "$RUN/planner.pid"

echo "[world] 启动 sidecar :8790（Yuki 进服）"
( cd "$ROOT/yuki-bot" && exec node sidecar/index.js ) > "$RUN/sidecar.log" 2>&1 &
echo $! > "$RUN/sidecar.pid"

for i in $(seq 1 90); do
  curl -sf http://127.0.0.1:8790/health 2>/dev/null | grep -q '"ready"' && break
  sleep 1
done
curl -sf http://127.0.0.1:8790/health >/dev/null && curl -sf http://127.0.0.1:8791/health >/dev/null || {
  echo "[world] 服务未就绪，见 $RUN/sidecar.log / $RUN/planner.log"; exit 1
}

# PID 文件以真实监听者为准（`( ... ) & echo $!` 可能拿到外层 subshell）
pid_on_port () { ss -lntp 2>/dev/null | grep ":$1 " | grep -o 'pid=[0-9]*' | head -1 | cut -d= -f2; }
pid_on_port 25565 > "$RUN/server.pid"
pid_on_port 8791 > "$RUN/planner.pid"
pid_on_port 8790 > "$RUN/sidecar.pid"

echo "[world] 就绪：客户端 26.1.2 离线模式连 127.0.0.1:25565，公屏带 yuki 关键字叫她"
echo "[world] 日志: $RUN/server.log  $RUN/sidecar.log  $RUN/planner.log"
echo "[world] 控制台命令: echo '/time set day' > $FIFO"

---

================
yuki-bot/scripts/chat-test.js

// 验证聊天指令闭环：第二个 bot 扮演玩家，喊 "yuki come" / "yuki stop"
const mineflayer = require('mineflayer')
const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms))
const opts = { host: '127.0.0.1', port: 25565, version: '26.1', auth: 'offline' }

;(async () => {
  const tester = mineflayer.createBot({ ...opts, username: 'Tester' })
  await new Promise((r) => tester.once('spawn', r))
  await sleep(1500)
  console.log('[tester] spawn @', tester.entity.position.toString())
  const yuki = () => Object.values(tester.entities).find((e) => e.username === 'Yuki')
  console.log('[tester] yuki visible:', !!yuki())
  tester.chat('yuki come')
  await sleep(6000)
  const d = yuki() ? yuki().position.distanceTo(tester.entity.position) : NaN
  console.log('[tester] distance after come:', d.toFixed(2))
  tester.chat('yuki pos')
  await sleep(1500)
  tester.chat('yuki stop')
  await sleep(1500)
  console.log('[tester] RESULT:', d < 4 ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL')
  tester.quit()
  process.exit(d < 4 ? 0 : 1)
})()

================
yuki-bot/scripts/compat-test.js
================

// 26.1 插件兼容性实测：逐个插件加载 + 实际调用，输出 PASS/FAIL
const mineflayer = require('mineflayer')
const { pathfinder, Movements } = require('mineflayer-pathfinder')
const { plugin: collectBlock } = require('mineflayer-collectblock')
const { plugin: pvp } = require('mineflayer-pvp').plugin ? require('mineflayer-pvp') : require('mineflayer-pvp')
const ItemFactory = require('prismarine-item')

const HOST = '127.0.0.1'
const PORT = 25566
const VER = '26.1'
const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms))
const results = []
const rec = (name, ok, detail) => { results.push({ name, ok, detail }); console.log(`${ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}  ${name}  ${detail}`) }

function loadPlugin (label, fn) {
  try { fn(); rec(`load:${label}`, true, 'loaded') } catch (e) { rec(`load:${label}`, false, e.message.split('\n')[0]) }
}

const bot = mineflayer.createBot({ host: HOST, port: PORT, username: 'Yuki', version: VER, auth: 'offline' })
const protocolWarnings = []
bot.on('kicked', (r) => console.log('KICKED:', JSON.stringify(r)))
bot.on('error', (e) => console.log('ERR:', e.message))
bot._client.on('error', (e) => protocolWarnings.push(e.message))

loadPlugin('pathfinder', () => bot.loadPlugin(pathfinder))
loadPlugin('collectblock', () => bot.loadPlugin(collectBlock))
loadPlugin('pvp', () => bot.loadPlugin(pvp))
loadPlugin('tool', () => bot.loadPlugin(require('mineflayer-tool').plugin))
loadPlugin('armor-manager', () => bot.loadPlugin(require('mineflayer-armor-manager')))
loadPlugin('auto-eat', () => bot.loadPlugin(require('mineflayer-auto-eat').loader))
// statemachine 1.7 不再提供 mineflayer plugin，纯库使用

bot.once('spawn', async () => {
  const mcData = require('minecraft-data')(bot.version)
  const Item = ItemFactory(bot.version)
  console.log(`spawned ${bot.version} @ ${bot.entity.position} gamemode=${bot.game.gameMode}`)
  try { bot.pathfinder.setMovements(new Movements(bot, mcData)) } catch (e) { rec('api:movements', false, e.message) }
  await sleep(1000)
  bot.chat('/difficulty easy')
  bot.chat('/gamerule doMobSpawning true')
  await sleep(800)

  // ---- 1. mineflayer-tool：按方块选工具
  try {
    await bot.creative.setInventorySlot(36, new Item(mcData.itemsByName.diamond_axe.id, 1))
    await sleep(300)
    const tp = bot.entity.position.floored().offset(2, 0, 3)
    bot.chat(`/setblock ${tp.x} ${tp.y} ${tp.z} minecraft:oak_log`)
    await sleep(1200)
    const log = bot.blockAt(tp)
    const tool = await bot.tool.equipForBlock(log)
    rec('api:tool.equipForBlock', !!tool && bot.heldItem?.name === 'diamond_axe', `picked=${tool?.name} held=${bot.heldItem?.name}`)
  } catch (e) { rec('api:tool.equipForBlock', false, e.message.split('\n')[0]) }

  // ---- 2. collectblock：挖掉该方块
  try {
    const tp = bot.entity.position.floored().offset(2, 0, 3)
    const log = bot.blockAt(tp)
    if (!log || log.name !== 'oak_log') throw new Error('target block missing: ' + (log && log.name))
    await bot.collectBlock.collect(log, { ignoreNoPath: true })
    const after = bot.blockAt(tp)
    rec('api:collectBlock.collect', after.name === 'air', `block now ${after.name}, inventory logs=${bot.inventory.items().filter(i => i.name.includes('log')).reduce((a, i) => a + i.count, 0)}`)
  } catch (e) { rec('api:collectBlock.collect', false, e.message.split('\n')[0]) }

  // ---- 3. pvp：打僵尸
  try {
    const before = Object.keys(bot.entities).length
    bot.chat('/summon zombie ~2 ~ ~2')
    await sleep(1500)
    const zombie = Object.values(bot.entities).find(e => e.name === 'zombie' || e.displayName === 'Zombie')
    if (!zombie) throw new Error(`no zombie spawned (entities ${before} -> ${Object.keys(bot.entities).length})`)
    bot.pvp.attack(zombie)
    const t0 = Date.now(); let dead = false
    while (Date.now() - t0 < 15000) {
      if (!bot.entities[zombie.id]) { dead = true; break }
      await sleep(300)
    }
    bot.pvp.stop()
    rec('api:pvp.attack', dead, dead ? `zombie killed in ${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(1)}s, hp=${bot.health}` : `zombie survived 15s, hp=${bot.health}`)
  } catch (e) { rec('api:pvp.attack', false, e.message.split('\n')[0]) }

  // ---- 4. armor-manager：自动穿装备
  try {
    await bot.creative.setInventorySlot(5, new Item(mcData.itemsByName.diamond_helmet.id, 1))
    await bot.creative.setInventorySlot(6, new Item(mcData.itemsByName.diamond_chestplate.id, 1))
    await sleep(1500)
    const head = bot.inventory.slots[5], chest = bot.inventory.slots[6]
    rec('api:armorManager', head?.name === 'diamond_helmet' && chest?.name === 'diamond_chestplate', `head=${head?.name} chest=${chest?.name}`)
  } catch (e) { rec('api:armorManager', false, e.message.split('\n')[0]) }

  // ---- 5. auto-eat：API 表面 + 启用不报错
  try {
    bot.autoEat.setOpts({ priority: 'foodPoints', startAt: 14 })
    bot.autoEat.enable()
    await sleep(500)
    rec('api:autoEat', typeof bot.autoEat.eat === 'function' && bot.autoEat.enabled === true, `enabled=${bot.autoEat.enabled} methods=${['eat', 'enable', 'disable', 'setOpts'].filter(m => typeof bot.autoEat[m] === 'function').join(',')}`)
    bot.autoEat.disable()
  } catch (e) { rec('api:autoEat', false, e.message.split('\n')[0]) }

  // ---- 6. statemachine：构造并运行一个状态机
  try {
    const { StateTransition, BotStateMachine, NestedStateMachine, BehaviorIdle } = require('mineflayer-statemachine')
    const idle = new BehaviorIdle()
    const transitions = [new StateTransition({ parent: idle, name: 'stay' })]
    const layer = new NestedStateMachine(transitions, idle)
    const sm = new BotStateMachine(bot, layer)
    await sleep(500)
    rec('api:statemachine', !!sm && typeof sm.start === 'function', `constructed, layer=${layer.constructor.name}, transitionClass=${typeof StateTransition}`)
    sm.stop()
  } catch (e) { rec('api:statemachine', false, e.message.split('\n')[0]) }

  // ---- 7. prismarine-viewer：无头环境渲染
  try {
    const { viewer } = require('prismarine-viewer')
    void viewer
    rec('api:prismarine-viewer.require', true, 'module required (渲染需 webgl/canvas，单独验证)')
  } catch (e) { rec('api:prismarine-viewer.require', false, e.message.split('\n')[0]) }

  // ---- 8. 26.1 协议健康度
  rec('proto:26.1-clean', protocolWarnings.length === 0, protocolWarnings.length ? protocolWarnings.slice(0, 2).join(' | ') : 'no protocol errors')

  console.log('\n=== SUMMARY ===')
  const fail = results.filter(r => !r.ok)
  console.log(`${results.length - fail.length}/${results.length} passed`)
  fail.forEach(f => console.log('  FAIL:', f.name, '-', f.detail))
  bot.quit(); process.exit(fail.length ? 1 : 0)
})

setTimeout(() => { console.log('TIMEOUT'); process.exit(3) }, 120000)
