================
yuki-bot/scripts/m0-test.js
================

'use strict'
// scripts/m0-test.js —— M0 验收脚本：模拟玩家在公屏喊话，断言全链路。
// 用法: node scripts/m0-test.js [--sidecar=http://127.0.0.1:8790] [--planner=http://127.0.0.1:8791]
const mineflayer = require('mineflayer')
const Vec3 = require('vec3')

const args = process.argv.slice(2)
const argOf = (name, fallback) => {
  const hit = args.find((a) => a.startsWith(`--${name}=`))
  return hit ? hit.split('=')[1] : fallback
}
const SIDECAR = argOf('sidecar', 'http://127.0.0.1:8790').replace(/\/+$/, '')
const PLANNER = argOf('planner', 'http://127.0.0.1:8791').replace(/\/+$/, '')
const MC_HOST = process.env.MC_HOST || '127.0.0.1'
const MC_PORT = parseInt(process.env.MC_PORT || '25565', 10)
const MC_VERSION = process.env.MC_VERSION || '26.1'
const TESTER = process.env.MC_TEST_USER || 'Tester'

const results = []
const said = []
const events = []
const waiters = []

function check (name, ok, detail) {
  results.push({ name, ok })
  console.log(`${ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} ${name}${detail ? ' | ' + JSON.stringify(detail) : ''}`)
}

function sleep (ms) { return new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms)) }

function onEvent (event) {
  events.push(event)
  if (event.type === 'say') {
    said.push(event)
    console.log(`  [say/${event.source}] ${event.text}`)
  } else if (['task_start', 'task_done', 'step_done', 'chat_in'].includes(event.type)) {
    console.log(`  [${event.type}] ${JSON.stringify(event).slice(0, 220)}`)
  }
  for (let i = waiters.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const waiter = waiters[i]
    if (waiter.predicate(event)) {
      waiters.splice(i, 1)
      clearTimeout(waiter.timer)
      waiter.resolve(event)
    }
  }
}

// 订阅 sidecar 的 SSE 事件流
async function subscribe () {
  const res = await fetch(`${SIDECAR}/events`, { headers: { Accept: 'text/event-stream' } })
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`sse_${res.status}`)
  const reader = res.body.getReader()
  const decoder = new TextDecoder()
  let buffer = ''
  ;(async () => {
    try {
      for (;;) {
        const { value, done } = await reader.read()
        if (done) break
        buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true })
        let idx
        while ((idx = buffer.indexOf('\n\n')) >= 0) {
          const chunk = buffer.slice(0, idx)
          buffer = buffer.slice(idx + 2)
          for (const line of chunk.split('\n')) {
            if (!line.startsWith('data: ')) continue
            try { onEvent(JSON.parse(line.slice(6))) } catch (_) { /* noop */ }
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (_) { /* stream closed */ }
  })()
}

function waitFor (predicate, timeoutMs, label) {
  const existing = events.find(predicate)
  if (existing) return Promise.resolve(existing)
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const waiter = { predicate, resolve }
    waiter.timer = setTimeout(() => {
      const i = waiters.indexOf(waiter)
      if (i >= 0) waiters.splice(i, 1)
      resolve(null)
    }, timeoutMs)
    waiters.push(waiter)
    console.log(`  … 等待 ${label} (${timeoutMs}ms)`)
  })
}

async function getJson (url) {
  const res = await fetch(url)
  return res.json()
}

async function main () {
  console.log(`== M0 验收 == sidecar=${SIDECAR} planner=${PLANNER} mc=${MC_HOST}:${MC_PORT}`)
  await subscribe()

  const state0 = await getJson(`${SIDECAR}/state`)
  check('L3 侧车 bot 在线', state0.bot === 'ready', { pos: state0.pos, hp: state0.health })
  const stats0 = await getJson(`${PLANNER}/stats`)

  const bot = mineflayer.createBot({
    host: MC_HOST, port: MC_PORT, username: TESTER, version: MC_VERSION, auth: 'offline'
  })
  bot.on('error', (err) => console.log('  tester error:', err.message))
  await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('tester spawn timeout')), 60000)
    bot.once('spawn', () => { clearTimeout(timer); resolve() })
  })
  console.log(`  玩家 ${TESTER} 已进入游戏 @ ${bot.entity.position}`)
  await sleep(1500)

  // 0) 环境预置校验：/fill 这类无返回命令必须用世界状态二次确认
  if (state0.pos && bot.registry) {
    const stoneId = bot.registry.blocksByName.stone.id
    const found = bot.findBlocks({
      point: new Vec3(Math.round(state0.pos.x), Math.round(state0.pos.y), Math.round(state0.pos.z)),
      matching: stoneId, maxDistance: 24, count: 64
    })
    check('环境预置：Yuki 附近有 ≥3 块石头', found.length >= 3, { nearby_stone: found.length })
  }

  // 1) 公屏喊话：她要用自己的语气回一句，且任务下发成功
  const t0 = Date.now()
  bot.chat('yuki 去挖点石头')
  const firstSay = await waitFor((e) => e.type === 'say' && e.source === 'chat', 30000, '她的第一句回话')
  check('① 公屏喊话后她回话（非空、无 JSON）', Boolean(firstSay && !/[{}"]/.test(firstSay.text)), {
    say: firstSay && firstSay.text, latency_ms: firstSay ? Date.now() - t0 : null
  })
  const taskStart = await waitFor((e) => e.type === 'task_start', 15000, '任务开始')
  check('① 任务成功下发到 L3', Boolean(taskStart), { steps: taskStart && taskStart.steps })
  // 下发这一轮的成本（听 + 规划 + 回话）先记下来，插话与汇报单独算
  const statsDispatch = await getJson(`${PLANNER}/stats`)

  // 2) 挖矿途中插话，验证不阻塞
  await sleep(5000)
  const tChat = Date.now()
  bot.chat('yuki 你在干嘛呀')
  const midSay = await waitFor(
    (e) => e.type === 'say' && e.source === 'chat' && e !== firstSay,
    20000, '挖矿途中的回话'
  )
  const doneBeforeMid = events.some((e) => e.type === 'task_done' && e.at <= (midSay ? midSay.at : Date.now()))
  check('④ 挖矿途中仍能回话（任务未结束）', Boolean(midSay) && !doneBeforeMid, {
    say: midSay && midSay.text, latency_ms: midSay ? Date.now() - tChat : null
  })

  // 3) 等任务收尾：硬校验结果 + 主动汇报
  const taskDone = await waitFor((e) => e.type === 'task_done', 240000, '任务终态')
  const firstResult = taskDone && taskDone.detail && taskDone.detail.results && taskDone.detail.results[0]
  const firstDetail = (firstResult && firstResult.detail) || {}
  check('② 任务拿到终态且状态 done', Boolean(taskDone) && taskDone.status === 'done', {
    status: taskDone && taskDone.status, summary: taskDone && taskDone.summary
  })
  check('② 背包增量 ≥ 3 石头（硬校验）', Boolean(firstResult) && Number(firstDetail.mined) >= 3, {
    mined: firstDetail.mined, requested: firstDetail.requested, block: firstDetail.block,
    item: firstDetail.item
  })

  const reportSay = await waitFor((e) => e.type === 'say' && e.source === 'callback', 45000, '主动汇报')
  check('③ 完成后主动汇报（自然语言、无 JSON/坐标）',
    Boolean(reportSay) && !/[{}"]/.test(reportSay.text) && !/\d+\.\d+\s+\d+\.\d+/.test(reportSay.text),
    { say: reportSay && reportSay.text })

  // 4) 预算：单次任务 LLM 调用 ≤ 4
  const stats1 = await getJson(`${PLANNER}/stats`)
  const deltaOf = (from, to) => {
    const out = {}
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(to.llm.by_purpose || {})) {
      out[key] = value - ((from.llm.by_purpose || {})[key] || 0)
    }
    return out
  }
  const dispatchDelta = deltaOf(stats0, statsDispatch)   // 听 -> 规划 -> 应一声
  const tailDelta = deltaOf(statsDispatch, stats1)       // 插话一次 + 汇报一次
  const taskCalls = Object.values(dispatchDelta).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) + (tailDelta.l1_report || 0)
  const interjectionCalls = (tailDelta.l1_chat || 0)
  check('⑤ 单次任务 LLM 调用 ≤ 4', taskCalls <= 4, { dispatch: dispatchDelta, report: tailDelta.l1_report || 0, task_calls: taskCalls, interjection_calls: interjectionCalls })

  // 5) 收尾：状态回空闲
  const state1 = await getJson(`${SIDECAR}/state`)
  check('任务结束后回到空闲态', state1.status === 'idle', { status: state1.status })

  bot.quit()
  const failed = results.filter((r) => !r.ok)
  console.log(`\n== 汇总: ${results.length - failed.length}/${results.length} 通过 ==`)
  if (said.length) console.log('她说过的所有话：\n  - ' + said.map((s) => s.text).join('\n  - '))
  process.exit(failed.length ? 1 : 0)
}

main().catch((err) => {
  console.error('测试脚本异常:', err.message)
  process.exit(2)
})

---

================
yuki-bot/scripts/p1-test.js
================

'use strict'
// scripts/p1-test.js —— P1 验收：纯脚本驱动，不调任何模型。
// 用法：
//   node scripts/p1-test.js [--sidecar=http://127.0.0.1:8790] [--fifo=/tmp/p1/server.in]
// fifo 存在时用服务端控制台预置场景（方块/箱子/怪/伤害/岩浆）；没有 fifo 时跳过这些场景。
const fs = require('fs')

const args = process.argv.slice(2)
const argOf = (name, fallback) => {
  const hit = args.find((a) => a.startsWith(`--${name}=`))
  return hit ? hit.split('=').slice(1).join('=') : fallback
}
const SIDECAR = argOf('sidecar', 'http://127.0.0.1:8790').replace(/\/+$/, '')
const FIFO = argOf('fifo', process.env.P1_FIFO || '')

const results = []
const events = []
const waiters = []
let fifoFd = null
let seq = 0

function check (name, ok, detail) {
  results.push({ name, ok })
  console.log(`${ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} ${name}${detail ? ' | ' + JSON.stringify(detail) : ''}`)
}

function sleep (ms) { return new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms)) }

function consoleCmd (line) {
  if (!FIFO) return false
  if (fifoFd === null) fifoFd = fs.openSync(FIFO, 'w')
  fs.writeSync(fifoFd, line + '\n')
  console.log(`  [console] ${line}`)
  return true
}

function onEvent (event) {
  events.push(event)
  for (let i = waiters.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const waiter = waiters[i]
    if (waiter.predicate(event)) {
      waiters.splice(i, 1)
      clearTimeout(waiter.timer)
      waiter.resolve(event)
    }
  }
}

async function subscribe () {
  const res = await fetch(`${SIDECAR}/events`, { headers: { Accept: 'text/event-stream' } })
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`sse_${res.status}`)
  const reader = res.body.getReader()
  const decoder = new TextDecoder()
  let buffer = ''
  ;(async () => {
    try {
      for (;;) {
        const { value, done } = await reader.read()
        if (done) break
        buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true })
        let idx
        while ((idx = buffer.indexOf('\n\n')) >= 0) {
          const chunk = buffer.slice(0, idx)
          buffer = buffer.slice(idx + 2)
          for (const line of chunk.split('\n')) {
            if (!line.startsWith('data: ')) continue
            try { onEvent(JSON.parse(line.slice(6))) } catch (_) { /* noop */ }
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (_) { /* closed */ }
  })()
}

function waitFor (predicate, timeoutMs, label) {
  const existing = events.find(predicate)
  if (existing) return Promise.resolve(existing)
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const waiter = { predicate, resolve }
    waiter.timer = setTimeout(() => {
      const i = waiters.indexOf(waiter)
      if (i >= 0) waiters.splice(i, 1)
      resolve(null)
    }, timeoutMs)
    waiters.push(waiter)
    if (label) console.log(`  … 等待 ${label} (${timeoutMs}ms)`)
  })
}

/** 只认调用之后新产生的事件，避免匹配到同类型的旧事件。 */
function waitNew (predicate, timeoutMs, label) {
  const start = events.length
  return waitFor((e) => events.indexOf(e) >= start && predicate(e), timeoutMs, label)
}

async function getJson (path) {
  const res = await fetch(`${SIDECAR}${path}`)
  return res.json()
}

async function post (path, body) {
  const res = await fetch(`${SIDECAR}${path}`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(body)
  })
  let json = {}
  try { json = await res.json() } catch (_) { /* noop */ }
  return { status: res.status, body: json }
}

async function state () { return getJson('/state') }

async function submit (steps, opts = {}) {
  const intentionId = opts.intention_id || `p1-${++seq}`
  const res = await post('/intention', {
    intention_id: intentionId,
    priority: opts.priority === undefined ? 5 : opts.priority,
    steps,
    origin: 'test'
  })
  return { ...res, intention_id: intentionId }
}

/** 提交一条意图并等它终态。 */
async function runIntention (steps, opts = {}) {
  const sub = await submit(steps, opts)
  if (!sub.body.accepted) return { sub, done: null }
  const done = await waitFor(
    (e) => e.type === 'task_done' && e.intention_id === sub.intention_id,
    opts.timeout || 90000,
    `任务 ${sub.intention_id} 终态`
  )
  return { sub, done }
}

async function waitHealth (min, timeoutMs = 8000) {
  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs
  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
    const s = await state()
    if (s.health >= min) return s
    await sleep(300)
  }
  return state()
}

async function waitFoodAtMost (max, timeoutMs = 15000) {
  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs
  let s = await state()
  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
    s = await state()
    if (typeof s.food === 'number' && s.food <= max) return s
    await sleep(250)
  }
  return s
}

function stepDetail (done, index) {
  return (done && done.detail && done.detail.results && done.detail.results[index] && done.detail.results[index].detail) || {}
}

// ---------------- 场景 ----------------
async function scenarioGotoRepeat (origin) {
  console.log('\n## 场景：空地 · 连续 10 次 goto')
  const offsets = [[3, 0], [-3, 0], [0, 3], [0, -3], [3, 3], [-3, -3], [3, -3], [-3, 3], [4, 0], [0, 4]]
  let ok = 0
  for (const [dx, dz] of offsets) {
    const { done } = await runIntention([{ skill: 'goto', args: { x: origin.x + dx, y: origin.y, z: origin.z + dz, range: 1 }, on_fail: 'abort' }], { timeout: 40000 })
    if (done && done.status === 'done') ok += 1
    else console.log(`   goto(${origin.x + dx},${origin.z + dz}) -> ${done ? done.status : 'no_done'}`)
  }
  check('① 连续 10 次 goto 成功率 ≥ 8/10', ok >= 8, { success: ok, total: 10 })
}

async function scenarioQueue (origin) {
  console.log('\n## 场景：队列语义（同级抢占 / 低优先级排队上限 / priority 9 拒收）')

  // 同级抢占：长任务进行中被同优先级任务顶掉
  const before = events.length
  const long = await submit([{ skill: 'goto', args: { x: origin.x + 24, y: origin.y, z: origin.z + 24, range: 1 } }], { priority: 5 })
  const started = await waitFor((e) => e.type === 'task_start' && e.intention_id === long.intention_id, 10000, '长任务开始')
  await sleep(600)
  const short = await submit([{ skill: 'goto', args: { x: origin.x + 2, y: origin.y, z: origin.z + 1, range: 1 } }], { priority: 5 })
  const preemptEvent = await waitFor((e) => e.type === 'preempt' && e.intention_id === long.intention_id, 5000, 'preempt 事件')
  const longDone = await waitFor((e) => e.type === 'task_done' && e.intention_id === long.intention_id, 15000, '旧任务终态')
  const shortDone = await waitFor((e) => e.type === 'task_done' && e.intention_id === short.intention_id, 60000, '新任务终态')
  check('② 同级抢占：旧任务 aborted、新任务完成',
    Boolean(started && preemptEvent) && longDone && longDone.status === 'aborted' && shortDone && shortDone.status === 'done',
    {
      preempt_event: Boolean(preemptEvent),
      old: longDone && longDone.status,
      new_task: shortDone && shortDone.status
    })
  void before

  // 低优先级排队上限 3
  const blocker = await submit([{ skill: 'goto', args: { x: origin.x + 20, y: origin.y, z: origin.z - 20, range: 1 } }], { priority: 6 })
  await waitFor((e) => e.type === 'task_start' && e.intention_id === blocker.intention_id, 10000, '占位任务开始')
  await sleep(300)
  const queued = []
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    queued.push(await submit([{ skill: 'goto', args: { x: origin.x + i, y: origin.y, z: origin.z - 2, range: 1 } }], { priority: 1 }))
  }
  const overflow = await submit([{ skill: 'goto', args: { x: origin.x + 1, y: origin.y, z: origin.z - 3, range: 1 } }], { priority: 1 })
  check('③ 低优先级排队上限 3（第 4 个 429 queue_full）',
    queued.every((q) => q.body.accepted && q.body.queued) && overflow.status === 429 && overflow.body.error === 'queue_full',
    { queued: queued.map((q) => q.body.queued), overflow: { status: overflow.status, error: overflow.body.error }, depth: queued.map((q) => q.body.depth) })

  // 取消占位任务，队列应清空
  await post('/cancel', { intention_id: blocker.intention_id, reason: 'test_cleanup' })
  for (const q of queued) {
    await waitFor((e) => e.type === 'task_done' && e.intention_id === q.intention_id, 30000, '排队任务跑完')
  }
  await sleep(300)

  // priority 9 直投必须被拒（守护逻辑只允许内部硬编码）
  const nine = await submit([{ skill: 'goto', args: { x: origin.x, y: origin.y, z: origin.z, range: 1 } }], { priority: 9 })
  check('④ priority=9 直投被拒（400 priority_out_of_range）',
    nine.status === 400 && nine.body.error === 'priority_out_of_range', { status: nine.status, error: nine.body.error })
}

async function scenarioCancel (origin) {
  console.log('\n## 场景：取消兜底复位')
  const sub = await submit([{ skill: 'goto', args: { x: origin.x + 26, y: origin.y, z: origin.z + 2, range: 1 } }], { priority: 4 })
  await waitFor((e) => e.type === 'task_start' && e.intention_id === sub.intention_id, 10000, '长任务开始')
  await sleep(800)
  const t0 = Date.now()
  await post('/cancel', { intention_id: sub.intention_id })
  let idle = null
  while (Date.now() - t0 < 1500) {
    const s = await state()
    if (s.status === 'idle' && !s.pathing) { idle = s; break }
    await sleep(100)
  }
  const aborted = await waitFor((e) => e.type === 'task_done' && e.intention_id === sub.intention_id && e.status === 'aborted', 5000, '取消终态')
  check('⑤ 取消后 1 秒内停住并回 idle', Boolean(idle) && Boolean(aborted), {
    latency_ms: idle ? Date.now() - t0 : null,
    state: idle && { status: idle.status, pathing: idle.pathing }
  })

  // 复位后还能继续干活（证明 goal/控制状态没卡死）
  const again = await runIntention([{ skill: 'goto', args: { x: origin.x, y: origin.y, z: origin.z, range: 1 } }], { timeout: 40000 })
  check('⑤ 取消后仍能正常执行下一条任务', Boolean(again.done && again.done.status === 'done'), { status: again.done && again.done.status })
}

async function scenarioForest (origin) {
  if (!FIFO) { console.log('\n## 场景：树林 —— 跳过（没有 fifo）'); return }
  console.log('\n## 场景：树林 · mine + craft')
  const bx = origin.x - 4
  const bz = origin.z + 3
  consoleCmd(`/fill ${bx} ${origin.y} ${bz} ${bx + 1} ${origin.y + 2} ${bz + 1} minecraft:oak_log`)
  await sleep(800)

  const { done } = await runIntention([
    { skill: 'mine', args: { block: 'oak_log', count: 3 }, on_fail: 'abort' },
    { skill: 'craft', args: { item: 'oak_planks', count: 8 }, on_fail: 'abort' },
    { skill: 'craft', args: { item: 'wooden_pickaxe', count: 1 }, on_fail: 'abort' }
  ], { timeout: 180000 })
  const mineDetail = stepDetail(done, 0)
  const craftDetail = stepDetail(done, 1)
  const pickDetail = stepDetail(done, 2)
  check('⑥ 树林：mine oak_log ≥3', Boolean(done && done.status === 'done') && mineDetail.mined >= 3, { mined: mineDetail.mined, blocks: done && done.status })
  check('⑦ craft 2×2 木板 ≥8（硬校验背包增量）', craftDetail.crafted >= 8, { crafted: craftDetail.crafted, item: craftDetail.item, error: craftDetail.error })
  check('⑧ 只用木板造出木镐（自动补木棍 + 工作台 3×3）',
    pickDetail.crafted >= 1 && Boolean(done && done.status === 'done'),
    { crafted: pickDetail.crafted, table: pickDetail.table, steps: pickDetail.steps, error: pickDetail.error })

  // place 技能单测：先做一张工作台，再放到脚边，硬校验方块真的出现了
  const placeRes = await runIntention([
    { skill: 'craft', args: { item: 'crafting_table', count: 1 }, on_fail: 'abort' },
    { skill: 'place', args: { item: 'crafting_table' }, on_fail: 'abort' }
  ], { timeout: 60000 })
  const placeDetail = stepDetail(placeRes.done, 1)
  check('⑧b place 放下工作台（方块出现硬校验）',
    Boolean(placeRes.done && placeRes.done.status === 'done') && placeDetail.placed === true,
    { placed: placeDetail.placed, at: placeDetail.at, error: placeDetail.error })
}

async function scenarioMine (origin) {
  if (!FIFO) { console.log('\n## 场景：矿洞 —— 跳过（没有 fifo）'); return }
  console.log('\n## 场景：矿洞 · mine stone')
  const bx = origin.x + 4
  const bz = origin.z - 3
  consoleCmd(`/fill ${bx} ${origin.y} ${bz} ${bx + 2} ${origin.y + 2} ${bz + 1} minecraft:stone`)
  await sleep(800)
  const { done } = await runIntention([{ skill: 'mine', args: { block: 'stone', count: 5 }, on_fail: 'abort' }], { timeout: 120000 })
  const d = stepDetail(done, 0)
  check('⑨ 矿洞：用自己造的镐 mine stone 5（查 drops 得 cobblestone）', Boolean(done && done.status === 'done') && d.mined >= 5, { mined: d.mined, item: d.item, requested: d.requested })
}

async function scenarioEquip (origin) {
  if (!FIFO) { console.log('\n## 场景：装备 —— 跳过（没有 fifo）'); return }
  console.log('\n## 场景：equip')
  consoleCmd('/give Yuki minecraft:diamond_sword 1')
  await sleep(600)
  const { done } = await runIntention([{ skill: 'equip', args: { item: 'diamond_sword' }, on_fail: 'abort' }], { timeout: 20000 })
  const d = stepDetail(done, 0)
  check('⑩ equip 拿在手上（硬校验槽位）', Boolean(done && done.status === 'done') && d.actual === 'diamond_sword', { actual: d.actual, error: d.error })
}

async function scenarioEat (origin) {
  if (!FIFO) { console.log('\n## 场景：进食 —— 跳过（没有 fifo）'); return }
  console.log('\n## 场景：eat')
  consoleCmd('/give Yuki minecraft:bread 3')
  // 饥饿效果 AMP 20 大约 2 秒掉 1 点饥饿，盯到 16 立刻清掉，别掉进守护阈值(6)
  consoleCmd('/effect give Yuki minecraft:hunger 30 20')
  const s = await waitFoodAtMost(16, 30000)
  consoleCmd('/effect clear Yuki minecraft:hunger')
  await sleep(500)
  console.log(`  food=${s.food} health=${s.health}`)
  const { done } = await runIntention([{ skill: 'eat', args: { food: 'bread' }, on_fail: 'abort' }], { timeout: 20000 })
  const d = stepDetail(done, 0)
  check('⑪ eat 后饥饿值真的上升', Boolean(done && done.status === 'done') && d.food_after > d.food_before, {
    before: d.food_before, after: d.food_after, item: d.item, error: d.error
  })
}

async function scenarioAttack (origin) {
  if (!FIFO) { console.log('\n## 场景：战斗 —— 跳过（没有 fifo）'); return }
  console.log('\n## 场景：attack')
  consoleCmd('/effect clear Yuki')
  consoleCmd('/effect give Yuki minecraft:instant_health 1 20')
  await waitHealth(18)
  const zx = origin.x + 9
  const zz = origin.z + 1
  consoleCmd(`/summon minecraft:zombie ${zx} ${origin.y} ${zz}`)
  await sleep(1200)
  const { done } = await runIntention([{ skill: 'attack', args: { target: 'zombie', count: 1 }, on_fail: 'abort' }], { timeout: 90000 })
  const d = stepDetail(done, 0)
  check('⑫ attack 打死僵尸（实体消失硬校验）', Boolean(done && done.status === 'done') && d.killed >= 1, {
    killed: d.killed, status: done && done.status, error: d.error
  })
  consoleCmd('/kill @e[type=minecraft:zombie]')
  await sleep(500)
}

async function scenarioDepositChain (origin) {
  if (!FIFO) { console.log('\n## 场景：存取链路 —— 跳过（没有 fifo）'); return }
  console.log('\n## 场景：mine → goto 家 → deposit')
  consoleCmd('/effect give Yuki minecraft:instant_health 1 20')
  await waitHealth(18)
  const bx = origin.x + 2
  const bz = origin.z - 3
  const cx = origin.x - 4
  const cz = origin.z - 4
  consoleCmd(`/fill ${bx} ${origin.y} ${bz} ${bx + 1} ${origin.y + 2} ${bz + 1} minecraft:stone`)
  consoleCmd(`/setblock ${cx} ${origin.y} ${cz} minecraft:chest`)
  await sleep(800)
  const before = await state()
  const cobble = (before.inventory || []).find((i) => i.name === 'cobblestone')
  const beforeCobble = cobble ? cobble.count : 0

  const { done } = await runIntention([
    { skill: 'mine', args: { block: 'stone', count: 5 }, on_fail: 'abort' },
    { skill: 'goto', args: { x: cx, y: origin.y, z: cz, range: 2 }, on_fail: 'abort' },
    { skill: 'deposit', args: { x: cx, y: origin.y, z: cz }, on_fail: 'abort' }
  ], { timeout: 180000 })
  const dep = stepDetail(done, 2)
  const after = await state()
  const afterCobble = (after.inventory || []).find((i) => i.name === 'cobblestone')
  check('⑬ 全链路 mine → goto 家 → deposit 完成', Boolean(done && done.status === 'done'), { status: done && done.status, summary: done && done.summary })
  check('⑬ deposit 硬校验：箱子收到东西、背包的圆石减少',
    dep.deposited > 0 && (afterCobble ? afterCobble.count : 0) < beforeCobble,
    { deposited: dep.deposited, container: dep.container, cobble_before: beforeCobble, cobble_after: afterCobble ? afterCobble.count : 0 })
}

async function scenarioGuardianHurt (origin) {
  if (!FIFO) { console.log('\n## 场景：守护逻辑 —— 跳过（没有 fifo）'); return }
  console.log('\n## 场景：守护逻辑 · 受击抢占（priority 9）')
  consoleCmd('/effect clear Yuki')
  consoleCmd('/effect give Yuki minecraft:instant_health 1 20')
  await waitHealth(18)
  // 先让食物回到高位，避免饥饿守护误触发
  consoleCmd('/effect give Yuki minecraft:saturation 10 10')
  await sleep(400)

  const sub = await submit([{ skill: 'goto', args: { x: origin.x + 30, y: origin.y, z: origin.z + 3, range: 1 } }], { priority: 5 })
  await waitFor((e) => e.type === 'task_start' && e.intention_id === sub.intention_id, 10000, '长任务开始')
  await sleep(1200)
  consoleCmd('/damage Yuki 8')
  const emergency = await waitNew((e) => e.type === 'emergency', 8000, 'emergency 事件')
  const aborted = await waitFor((e) => e.type === 'task_done' && e.intention_id === sub.intention_id, 15000, '被抢占任务终态')
  check('⑭ 受击触发守护抢占（emergency + 任务 aborted）',
    Boolean(emergency) && emergency.kind === 'hurt' && aborted && aborted.status === 'aborted' && String(aborted.reason || '').startsWith('guardian:'),
    { kind: emergency && emergency.kind, status: aborted && aborted.status, reason: aborted && aborted.reason })

  // 等守护动作收尾
  await sleep(4000)
  consoleCmd('/effect give Yuki minecraft:instant_health 1 20')

  // 低血阈值
  const sub2 = await submit([{ skill: 'goto', args: { x: origin.x - 30, y: origin.y, z: origin.z + 3, range: 1 } }], { priority: 5 })
  await waitFor((e) => e.type === 'task_start' && e.intention_id === sub2.intention_id, 10000, '长任务开始')
  await sleep(1200)
  consoleCmd('/damage Yuki 16')
  const emergency2 = await waitNew((e) => e.type === 'emergency' && e.kind === 'low_health', 8000, 'low_health 事件')
  const aborted2 = await waitFor((e) => e.type === 'task_done' && e.intention_id === sub2.intention_id, 15000, '被抢占任务终态')
  check('⑮ 低血阈值触发守护（low_health + aborted）',
    Boolean(emergency2) && aborted2 && aborted2.status === 'aborted',
    { kind: emergency2 && emergency2.kind, status: aborted2 && aborted2.status })
  await sleep(4000)
  consoleCmd('/effect clear Yuki')
  consoleCmd('/effect give Yuki minecraft:instant_health 1 20')
}

async function scenarioGuardianLava (origin) {
  if (!FIFO) { console.log('\n## 场景：岩浆 —— 跳过（没有 fifo）'); return }
  console.log('\n## 场景：守护逻辑 · 脚下岩浆')
  const s = await state()
  const p = s.pos
  consoleCmd(`/setblock ${Math.floor(p.x)} ${Math.floor(p.y)} ${Math.floor(p.z)} minecraft:lava`)
  const emergency = await waitNew((e) => e.type === 'emergency' && e.kind === 'lava', 8000, 'lava 事件')
  await sleep(6000)
  const after = await state()
  const blocks = after.pos
  let inLava = true
  if (blocks) {
    // 用 Tester 视角不可靠，直接看状态：活着且位置变了/血量 > 0 即视为逃出
    inLava = Math.floor(blocks.x) === Math.floor(p.x) && Math.floor(blocks.z) === Math.floor(p.z)
  }
  check('⑯ 脚下岩浆触发守护并逃生', Boolean(emergency) && after.health > 0 && !inLava, {
    kind: emergency && emergency.kind, health: after.health, from: p, to: blocks
  })
  consoleCmd(`/setblock ${Math.floor(p.x)} ${Math.floor(p.y)} ${Math.floor(p.z)} minecraft:air`)
  consoleCmd('/effect give Yuki minecraft:instant_health 1 20')
}

// ---------------- 主流程 ----------------
async function main () {
  console.log(`== P1 验收 == sidecar=${SIDECAR} fifo=${FIFO || '(none)'}`)
  await subscribe()
  const s0 = await state()
  check('L3 侧车 bot 在线', s0.bot === 'ready', { pos: s0.pos, hp: s0.health, food: s0.food })
  if (!s0.pos) { console.log('bot 没有位置信息，退出'); process.exit(2) }
  let origin = { x: Math.round(s0.pos.x), y: Math.round(s0.pos.y), z: Math.round(s0.pos.z) }

  if (FIFO) {
    consoleCmd('/gamerule spawn_monsters false')
    consoleCmd('/time set day')
    consoleCmd('/clear Yuki')
    consoleCmd('/weather clear')
    // 搭一块 13×13 平台并在上面清空，保证「空地」场景与传送不再受出生点地形影响
    const base = { x: origin.x, y: origin.y + 2, z: origin.z }
    consoleCmd(`/fill ${base.x - 7} ${base.y} ${base.z - 7} ${base.x + 7} ${base.y} ${base.z + 7} minecraft:stone`)
    consoleCmd(`/fill ${base.x - 7} ${base.y + 1} ${base.z - 7} ${base.x + 7} ${base.y + 5} ${base.z + 7} minecraft:air`)
    consoleCmd(`/tp Yuki ${base.x + 0.5} ${base.y + 1.2} ${base.z + 0.5}`)
    await sleep(2000)
    const s1 = await state()
    if (s1.pos) origin = { x: Math.round(base.x), y: Math.round(base.y + 1), z: Math.round(base.z) }
    console.log(`  测试平台: ${JSON.stringify(origin)}`)
  }

  await scenarioGotoRepeat(origin)
  await scenarioQueue(origin)
  await scenarioCancel(origin)
  await scenarioForest(origin)
  await scenarioMine(origin)
  await scenarioEquip(origin)
  await scenarioEat(origin)
  await scenarioAttack(origin)
  await scenarioDepositChain(origin)
  await scenarioGuardianHurt(origin)
  await scenarioGuardianLava(origin)

  const failed = results.filter((r) => !r.ok)
  console.log(`\n== 汇总: ${results.length - failed.length}/${results.length} 通过 ==`)
  if (failed.length) console.log('失败项:\n  - ' + failed.map((r) => r.name).join('\n  - '))
  process.exit(failed.length ? 1 : 0)
}

main().catch((err) => {
  console.error('测试脚本异常:', err.stack || err.message)
  process.exit(2)
})

---

================
yuki-bot/scripts/p2-test.js
================

'use strict'
// scripts/p2-test.js —— P2 验收：L2 规划器接 9 技能、10 次「挖 20 圆石」、不可达目标升级、非法 JSON 率、预算。
// 用法：
//   node scripts/p2-test.js [--sidecar=http://127.0.0.1:8790] [--planner=http://127.0.0.1:8791] [--fifo=/tmp/p2/server.in]
// 需要主服 :25565、sidecar :8790、planner :8791 同时在跑；fifo 用于服务端控制台预置场景。
const fs = require('fs')
const mineflayer = require('mineflayer')

const args = process.argv.slice(2)
const argOf = (name, fallback) => {
  const hit = args.find((a) => a.startsWith(`--${name}=`))
  return hit ? hit.split('=').slice(1).join('=') : fallback
}
const SIDECAR = argOf('sidecar', 'http://127.0.0.1:8790').replace(/\/+$/, '')
const PLANNER = argOf('planner', 'http://127.0.0.1:8791').replace(/\/+$/, '')
const FIFO = argOf('fifo', process.env.P2_FIFO || '')
const ONLY = argOf('only', 'all') // all | nl | mine | follow | diamond
const MC_HOST = process.env.MC_HOST || '127.0.0.1'
const MC_PORT = parseInt(process.env.MC_PORT || '25565', 10)
const MC_VERSION = process.env.MC_VERSION || '26.1'
const TESTER = process.env.MC_TEST_USER || 'Tester'

const results = []
const events = []
const waiters = []
let fifoFd = null

function check (name, ok, detail) {
  results.push({ name, ok })
  console.log(`${ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} ${name}${detail ? ' | ' + JSON.stringify(detail) : ''}`)
}

function sleep (ms) { return new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms)) }

function consoleCmd (line) {
  if (!FIFO) return false
  if (fifoFd === null) fifoFd = fs.openSync(FIFO, 'w')
  fs.writeSync(fifoFd, line + '\n')
  return true
}

function onEvent (event) {
  events.push(event)
  if (['task_start', 'task_done', 'step_retry', 'say', 'preempt'].includes(event.type)) {
    const echo = { ...event }
    if (echo.steps) echo.steps = echo.steps.map((s) => `${s.skill}(${JSON.stringify(s.args)})`).join(' > ')
    if (echo.detail) echo.detail = undefined
    console.log(`  [${event.type}] ${JSON.stringify(echo).slice(0, 240)}`)
  }
  for (let i = waiters.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const waiter = waiters[i]
    if (waiter.predicate(event)) {
      waiters.splice(i, 1)
      clearTimeout(waiter.timer)
      waiter.resolve(event)
    }
  }
}

async function subscribe () {
  const res = await fetch(`${SIDECAR}/events`, { headers: { Accept: 'text/event-stream' } })
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`sse_${res.status}`)
  const reader = res.body.getReader()
  const decoder = new TextDecoder()
  let buffer = ''
  ;(async () => {
    try {
      for (;;) {
        const { value, done } = await reader.read()
        if (done) break
        buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true })
        let idx
        while ((idx = buffer.indexOf('\n\n')) >= 0) {
          const chunk = buffer.slice(0, idx)
          buffer = buffer.slice(idx + 2)
          for (const line of chunk.split('\n')) {
            if (!line.startsWith('data: ')) continue
            try { onEvent(JSON.parse(line.slice(6))) } catch (_) { /* noop */ }
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (_) { /* closed */ }
  })()
}

/** 只匹配调用之后新出现的事件 */
function waitNew (predicate, timeoutMs, label) {
  const start = events.length
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const hit = events.slice(start).find(predicate)
    if (hit) return resolve(hit)
    const waiter = { predicate: (e) => events.indexOf(e) >= start && predicate(e), resolve }
    waiter.timer = setTimeout(() => {
      const i = waiters.indexOf(waiter)
      if (i >= 0) waiters.splice(i, 1)
      resolve(null)
    }, timeoutMs)
    waiters.push(waiter)
    if (label) console.log(`  … 等待 ${label} (${timeoutMs}ms)`)
  })
}

async function getJson (url) {
  const res = await fetch(url)
  return res.json()
}

async function postJson (url, body) {
  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(body)
  })
  let json = {}
  try { json = await res.json() } catch (_) { /* noop */ }
  return { status: res.status, body: json }
}

async function state () { return getJson(`${SIDECAR}/state`) }
async function stats () { return getJson(`${PLANNER}/stats`) }

async function waitIdle (timeoutMs) {
  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs
  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
    const s = await state()
    if (s.status === 'idle' && !s.current) return s
    await sleep(400)
  }
  return state()
}

async function cancelCurrent (reason) {
  const s = await state()
  if (s.current && s.current.intention_id) {
    await postJson(`${SIDECAR}/cancel`, { intention_id: s.current.intention_id, reason })
  }
}

// ---------------- 场景：NL → 9 技能 ----------------
const NL_CASES = [
  { text: '去挖20个圆石', want: 'mine', match: (s) => s.skill === 'mine' && ['stone', 'cobblestone'].includes(String(s.args.block)) && Number(s.args.count) === 20 },
  { text: '去砍三块木头', want: 'mine', match: (s) => s.skill === 'mine' && String(s.args.block).includes('log') },
  { text: '跟着 Tester 别走丢', want: 'follow', match: (s) => s.skill === 'follow' },
  { text: '把背包里的圆石存到最近的箱子里', want: 'deposit', match: (s) => s.skill === 'deposit' },
  { text: '去打附近那只僵尸', want: 'attack', match: (s) => s.skill === 'attack' },
  { text: '把钻石剑拿在手上', want: 'equip', match: (s) => s.skill === 'equip' && String(s.args.item).includes('sword') },
  { text: '把工作台放在脚边', want: 'place', match: (s) => s.skill === 'place' },
  { text: '我饿了，吃点东西', want: 'eat', match: (s) => s.skill === 'eat' },
  { text: '造一把木镐', want: 'craft', match: (s) => s.skill === 'craft' && String(s.args.item).includes('pickaxe') },
  { text: '走到坐标 10 64 10', want: 'goto', match: (s) => s.skill === 'goto' },
  { text: '过来我这里', want: 'goto', match: (s) => s.skill === 'goto' }
]

async function scenarioNlCoverage () {
  console.log('\n## 场景：自然语言 → 9 技能（直连 /plan）')
  let ok = 0
  const misses = []
  for (const testCase of NL_CASES) {
    const res = await postJson(`${PLANNER}/plan`, { text: testCase.text })
    const steps = (res.body && res.body.steps) || []
    const hit = steps.some(testCase.match)
    if (hit) ok += 1
    else misses.push({ text: testCase.text, want: testCase.want, got: steps })
  }
  check('① 11 句自然语言覆盖全部 9 技能（含「过来」→goto 玩家坐标）', ok >= NL_CASES.length - 1, { passed: ok, total: NL_CASES.length, misses })
}

// ---------------- 场景：10 次「挖 20 圆石」（L2 规划 + L3 执行，不过 L1） ----------------
async function scenarioMine20 (origin) {
  console.log('\n## 场景：10 次「去挖20个圆石」（/plan → /intention）')
  const ox = Math.round(origin.x)
  const oy = Math.round(origin.y)
  const oz = Math.round(origin.z)
  const qx = ox + 2
  const qz = oz - 2
  let success = 0
  let plannedOk = 0
  let nullBlocks = 0
  const failedRounds = []
  const planMs = []

  for (let round = 1; round <= 10; round++) {
    consoleCmd('/clear Yuki minecraft:cobblestone')
    consoleCmd(`/fill ${qx} ${oy} ${qz} ${qx + 4} ${oy + 2} ${qz + 4} minecraft:stone`)
    consoleCmd('/effect give Yuki minecraft:instant_health 1 20')
    await sleep(700)

    const t0 = Date.now()
    const planRes = await postJson(`${PLANNER}/plan`, { text: '去挖20个圆石' })
    planMs.push(Date.now() - t0)
    const steps = (planRes.body && planRes.body.steps) || []
    const first = steps[0] || {}
    const planOk = planRes.status === 200 && first.skill === 'mine' && Number(first.args.count) === 20
    if (planOk) plannedOk += 1
    if (first.args && first.args.block === 'cobblestone') nullBlocks += 1

    const intentionId = `p2-mine-${round}-${Date.now()}`
    const submit = await postJson(`${SIDECAR}/intention`, {
      intention_id: intentionId, priority: 5, origin: 'test', steps, summary_text: '去挖20个圆石'
    })
    if (!submit.body.accepted) {
      failedRounds.push({ round, reason: 'not_accepted', body: submit.body })
      continue
    }
    const done = await waitNew((e) => e.type === 'task_done' && e.intention_id === intentionId, 210000, `第${round}轮终态`)
    const mined = done && done.detail && done.detail.results && done.detail.results[0] &&
      done.detail.results[0].detail && done.detail.results[0].detail.mined
    if (planOk && done && done.status === 'done' && Number(mined) >= 20) {
      success += 1
      console.log(`  第 ${round} 轮 OK：${first.args.block} ×${mined} ${Date.now() - t0}ms`)
    } else {
      failedRounds.push({ round, plan: steps, status: done && done.status, mined })
      console.log(`  第 ${round} 轮 FAIL：plan=${JSON.stringify(steps)} status=${done && done.status} mined=${mined}`)
      if (!done) await cancelCurrent('test_timeout')
    }
    await waitIdle(20000)
  }

  const avgPlan = Math.round(planMs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / (planMs.length || 1))
  check('② 「去挖20个圆石」成功 ≥ 7/10', success >= 7, { success, total: 10, avg_plan_ms: avgPlan })
  check('② 10 次规划全部合法（mine count=20）', plannedOk === 10, { plannedOk, failedRounds })
  check('② 圆石请求默认挖 stone（不误挑 cobblestone）', nullBlocks === 0, { cobblestone_plans: nullBlocks })
}

// ---------------- 场景：持续跟随（不给 seconds）+ 随时改派 + 记忆释放 ----------------
async function scenarioFollow () {
  console.log('\n## 场景：持续跟随 / 随时改派 / 结束后释放工作记忆')
  const bot = global.testerBot
  const before = events.length

  // 让 Tester 走开 15 格，确保有得跟
  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) { bot.setControlState('forward', true); await sleep(150) }
  bot.setControlState('forward', false)
  await sleep(800)

  const dispatched = await postJson(`${PLANNER}/dispatch`, { text: `跟着 ${TESTER}，别跟丢了`, priority: 3 })
  const followId = dispatched.body && dispatched.body.intention_id
  check('⑤ 持续跟随成功下发', Boolean(followId), { status: dispatched.status, body: dispatched.body })
  if (!followId) return
  const step = (dispatched.body && dispatched.body.steps && dispatched.body.steps[0]) || null
  check('⑤ L2 对「跟着我」不给 seconds（开放跟随）',
    Boolean(step && step.skill === 'follow' && (step.args.seconds === undefined || step.args.seconds === null)),
    { step })
  await waitNew((e) => e.type === 'task_start' && e.intention_id === followId, 20000, 'follow 开始')

  // 等 20 秒（超过旧的 15 秒默认值），她必须还在跟
  await sleep(20000)
  const during = await state()
  check('⑤ 20 秒后仍在跟随（不被默认时长提前结束）',
    during.status === 'working' && Boolean(during.current) && during.current.intention_id === followId,
    { status: during.status, current: during.current })

  // 随时改派：新委托抢占，跟随即 aborted
  const revised = await postJson(`${PLANNER}/dispatch`, { text: `到 ${TESTER} 那里去`, priority: 5 })
  const revisedId = revised.body && revised.body.intention_id
  const oldDone = await waitNew((e) => e.type === 'task_done' && e.intention_id === followId, 20000, 'follow 被抢占')
  // 抢占只要求新任务真的起来了；它自己能否走通不属于本条验收（地形可能不可达）
  // 新任务可能在 await oldDone 期间就已经开始并结束了，先查已发生的事件
  const newStart = events.slice(before).find((e) => e.type === 'task_start' && e.intention_id === revisedId) ||
    await waitNew((e) => e.type === 'task_start' && e.intention_id === revisedId, 20000, '新任务开始')
  check('⑤ 新委托直接抢占持续跟随',
    Boolean(oldDone && oldDone.status === 'aborted' && String(oldDone.reason || '').includes('preempt') && newStart),
    { old: oldDone && oldDone.status, reason: oldDone && oldDone.reason, new_started: Boolean(newStart) })

  await waitIdle(30000)
  await sleep(3000)
  const statsAfter = await stats()
  check('⑤ 任务结束后释放工作记忆（chains 归零）', (statsAfter.chains || 0) === 0, { chains: statsAfter.chains })
}

// ---------------- 场景：不可达目标（远古残骸只在猪灵维度，主世界必然挖不到） ----------------
async function scenarioUnreachable () {
  console.log('\n## 场景：不可达目标「去挖二十块远古残骸」（/dispatch → 失败升级）')
  const before = events.length
  const statsBefore = await stats()
  const t0 = Date.now()
  const dispatched = await postJson(`${PLANNER}/dispatch`, { text: '去挖二十块远古残骸', priority: 5 })
  const intentionId = dispatched.body && dispatched.body.intention_id
  check('③ 不可达委托成功下发（/dispatch）', Boolean(intentionId), { status: dispatched.status, body: dispatched.body })
  if (!intentionId) return

  // 等到 L1 收工（idle 且有一段安静期）或超时
  let idle = null
  const deadline = Date.now() + 180000
  let lastChange = Date.now()
  let lastSnapshot = ''
  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
    const s = await state()
    const queueLen = (s.queue || []).length
    const snap = `${s.status}:${s.current ? s.current.intention_id : 'null'}:${queueLen}`
    if (snap !== lastSnapshot) { lastSnapshot = snap; lastChange = Date.now() }
    if (s.status === 'idle' && !s.current && queueLen === 0 && Date.now() - lastChange > 6000) {
      idle = s
      break
    }
    await sleep(500)
  }
  const newEvents = events.slice(before)
  const starts = newEvents.filter((e) => e.type === 'task_start')
  const debrisStarts = starts.filter((e) => (e.steps || []).some((step) => /debris|ancient/.test(String((step.args || {}).block || ''))))
  const says = newEvents.filter((e) => e.type === 'say' && e.source === 'callback')
  const lastSay = says[says.length - 1]
  const tasksDone = newEvents.filter((e) => e.type === 'task_done')

  const after = await stats()
  const replans = (after.llm.by_purpose.l2_replan || 0) - (statsBefore.llm.by_purpose.l2_replan || 0)
  const optionsCalls = (after.llm.by_purpose.l2_options || 0) - (statsBefore.llm.by_purpose.l2_options || 0)
  const chain = (after.recent || [])[after.recent.length - 1] || {}

  check('③ 不可达目标不死循环：最终回到 idle（≤180s）', Boolean(idle), {
    elapsed_ms: Date.now() - t0, status: idle && idle.status
  })
  check('③ L2 自动重规划 2 轮后才交给 L1', replans === 2, { replans, debris_tasks: debrisStarts.length, tasks: starts.length })
  check('③ L1 拿到事实 + 方案并做过一次决断', chain.negotiated === true && optionsCalls >= 1,
    { chain, options_calls: optionsCalls })
  check('③ 有人话原因且不含 JSON/坐标', Boolean(lastSay && lastSay.text && !/[{}"[\]]/.test(lastSay.text)),
    { say: lastSay && lastSay.text, tasks_done: tasksDone.length })
  check('③ 没有把任务卡在做不完的状态里（queue 空）', Boolean(idle && !(idle.current) && (idle.queue || []).length === 0),
    { queue: idle && (idle.queue || []).length, current: idle && idle.current })
}

// ---------------- 场景：非法 JSON 率与预算 ----------------
async function scenarioMetrics (statsBefore) {
  console.log('\n## 场景：非法 JSON 率与单任务预算')
  const after = await stats()
  const plan = after.plan || {}
  const attempts = plan.attempts || 0
  const rate = plan.invalid_rate || 0
  const deltaAttempts = attempts - (statsBefore.plan ? statsBefore.plan.attempts : 0)
  const allowed = ['mine', 'goto', 'follow', 'craft', 'deposit', 'attack', 'eat', 'equip', 'place']
  const leaked = events.filter((e) => e.type === 'task_start' &&
    (e.steps || []).some((step) => !allowed.includes(step.skill)))
  check('④ 非法 JSON 率 < 5%（真实调用统计）', attempts > 0 && rate < 0.05, {
    attempts, invalid: plan.invalid, rate, delta_attempts: deltaAttempts
  })
  check('④ 非法输出全部被拦截（执行器从未收到非法技能）', leaked.length === 0, {
    rejected_by_schema: plan.rejected, repairs: plan.repairs, leaked: leaked.length
  })
  const budget = after.last_budget || {}
  const within = budget.calls <= budget.max_calls && budget.tokens <= budget.max_tokens && budget.retries <= budget.max_retries
  check('④ 单任务预算生效（calls/tokens/retries 未超限）', Boolean(budget.max_calls) && within, budget)
}

// ---------------- 主流程 ----------------
async function main () {
  console.log(`== P2 验收 == sidecar=${SIDECAR} planner=${PLANNER} mc=${MC_HOST}:${MC_PORT}`)
  await subscribe()

  const state0 = await state()
  check('L3 侧车 bot 在线', state0.bot === 'ready', { pos: state0.pos, hp: state0.health })
  if (!state0.bot || state0.bot !== 'ready') process.exit(1)
  const origin = state0.pos

  if (FIFO) {
    const ox = Math.round(origin.x)
    const oy = Math.round(origin.y)
    const oz = Math.round(origin.z)
    consoleCmd('/difficulty peaceful')
    consoleCmd('/gamerule spawn_monsters false')
    consoleCmd('/gamerule keep_inventory true')
    consoleCmd('/time set day')
    consoleCmd('/weather clear')
    consoleCmd('/kill @e[type=minecraft:zombie]')
    consoleCmd('/kill @e[type=minecraft:skeleton]')
    consoleCmd('/kill @e[type=minecraft:spider]')
    consoleCmd('/kill @e[type=minecraft:creeper]')
    // 把她脚边清空，避免卡在旧测试遗留的方块里吃窒息伤害（会误触受击守护）
    consoleCmd(`/fill ${ox - 1} ${oy} ${oz - 1} ${ox + 1} ${oy + 2} ${oz + 1} minecraft:air`)
    consoleCmd('/effect clear Yuki')
    consoleCmd('/effect give Yuki minecraft:instant_health 1 20')
    consoleCmd('/effect give Yuki minecraft:saturation 10 10')
    consoleCmd('/give Yuki minecraft:diamond_pickaxe 1')
    consoleCmd('/give Yuki minecraft:diamond_sword 1')
    await sleep(1500)
  }

  const bot = mineflayer.createBot({
    host: MC_HOST, port: MC_PORT, username: TESTER, version: MC_VERSION, auth: 'offline'
  })
  global.testerBot = bot
  bot.on('error', (err) => console.log('  tester error:', err.message))
  await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('tester spawn timeout')), 60000)
    bot.once('spawn', () => { clearTimeout(timer); resolve() })
  })
  console.log(`  玩家 ${TESTER} 已进入游戏`)
  await sleep(1200)

  const statsBefore = await stats()
  if (ONLY === 'all' || ONLY === 'nl') await scenarioNlCoverage()
  if (ONLY === 'all' || ONLY === 'mine') await scenarioMine20(origin)
  if (ONLY === 'all' || ONLY === 'follow') await scenarioFollow()
  if (ONLY === 'all' || ONLY === 'diamond') {
    await scenarioUnreachable()
    await scenarioMetrics(statsBefore)
  }

  // 收尾：清掉测试怪、回血
  if (FIFO) {
    consoleCmd('/effect give Yuki minecraft:instant_health 1 20')
    consoleCmd('/kill @e[type=minecraft:zombie]')
  }
  bot.quit()

  const passed = results.filter((r) => r.ok).length
  console.log(`\n== P2 验收结果：${passed}/${results.length} ==`)
  for (const r of results) if (!r.ok) console.log(`  FAIL ${r.name}`)
  process.exit(passed === results.length ? 0 : 1)
}

main().catch((err) => {
  console.error('p2-test crash:', err)
  process.exit(1)
})

================
yuki-bot/scripts/probe-262.js
================

'use strict'
// scripts/probe-262.js —— 26.2（协议 776）连通性探针。
// 用途：上游还没给 26.2 协议数据时，验证「拿 26.1 数据充 26.2」能不能真的登录、走路、挖方块。
// 用法：node scripts/probe-262.js [host] [port]
const mineflayer = require('mineflayer')

const HOST = process.argv[2] || '127.0.0.1'
const PORT = Number(process.argv[3] || 25568)
const VERSION = process.env.PROBE_VERSION || '26.2'

function log (event, extra) {
  const t = new Date().toISOString().slice(11, 19)
  console.log(`${t} ${event}${extra ? ' ' + JSON.stringify(extra) : ''}`)
}

const bot = mineflayer.createBot({
  host: HOST,
  port: PORT,
  username: process.env.PROBE_USER || 'YukiProbe',
  version: VERSION,
  auth: 'offline'
})

let digDone = false
const timer = setTimeout(() => {
  log('timeout', { spawned: bot.entity != null, digDone })
  process.exit(bot.entity ? 2 : 1)
}, 60000)

bot.on('error', (err) => log('error', { message: err.message }))
bot.on('kicked', (reason) => log('kicked', { reason: typeof reason === 'string' ? reason : JSON.stringify(reason) }))
bot.on('end', (reason) => log('end', { reason: String(reason) }))
bot.on('messagestr', (msg) => log('server_message', { msg: msg.slice(0, 120) }))

bot.once('spawn', async () => {
  log('spawn', { version: bot.version, entityId: bot.entity.id, pos: round(bot.entity.position) })
  try {
    const mcData = require('minecraft-data')(bot.version)
    log('mcData', { protocol: mcData.version.version, blocks: Object.keys(mcData.blocksByName).length, items: Object.keys(mcData.itemsByName).length })

    // 1) 读脚下的方块（方块状态解码）
    const feet = bot.blockAt(bot.entity.position.offset(0, -1, 0))
    log('block_below', { name: feet ? feet.name : null, id: feet ? feet.stateId : null })

    // 2) 走路（实体/物理/移动包）
    bot.setControlState('forward', true)
    await wait(1200)
    bot.setControlState('forward', false)
    log('moved', { pos: round(bot.entity.position) })

    // 3) 挖一格（方块告警/挖掘包/掉落物）
    const target = bot.blockAt(bot.entity.position.offset(1, -1, 0))
    if (target && target.name !== 'air' && target.diggable !== false) {
      await bot.dig(target)
      digDone = true
      log('dig_ok', { block: target.name })
    } else {
      log('dig_skipped', { name: target && target.name })
    }

    // 4) 背包（窗口包 / inventory 序列化）
    log('inventory', { items: bot.inventory.items().map((i) => `${i.name}x${i.count}`).slice(0, 8) })
    log('health', { health: bot.health, food: bot.food })
    log('result', { ok: true, digDone })
    clearTimeout(timer)
    bot.quit()
    setTimeout(() => process.exit(0), 500)
  } catch (err) {
    log('probe_failed', { message: err.message, stack: (err.stack || '').split('\n')[1] })
    clearTimeout(timer)
    bot.quit()
    setTimeout(() => process.exit(3), 500)
  }
})

function wait (ms) { return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms)) }
function round (p) { return { x: Math.round(p.x * 10) / 10, y: Math.round(p.y * 10) / 10, z: Math.round(p.z * 10) / 10 } }

================
yuki-bot/scripts/yuki.js
================

// Yuki bot 最小闭环：登录 -> 状态上报 -> 寻路/跟随（L3 运动皮层）
// 用法: node scripts/yuki.js [--selftest]
const mineflayer = require('mineflayer')
const { pathfinder, Movements, goals } = require('mineflayer-pathfinder')

const HOST = process.env.MC_HOST || '127.0.0.1'
const PORT = parseInt(process.env.MC_PORT || '25565', 10)
const USER = process.env.MC_USER || 'Yuki'
const VERSION = process.env.MC_VERSION || '26.1'
const SELFTEST = process.argv.includes('--selftest')

const log = (...a) => console.log(`[yuki ${new Date().toISOString().slice(11, 19)}]`, ...a)
const fmt = (p) => p ? `${p.x.toFixed(1)} ${p.y.toFixed(1)} ${p.z.toFixed(1)}` : 'null'

const bot = mineflayer.createBot({ host: HOST, port: PORT, username: USER, version: VERSION, auth: 'offline' })
bot.loadPlugin(pathfinder)

let followTarget = null
let followTimer = null

bot.once('spawn', () => {
  const mcData = require('minecraft-data')(bot.version)
  bot.pathfinder.setMovements(new Movements(bot, mcData))
  log(`spawned as ${bot.username} @ ${fmt(bot.entity.position)} (${bot.version})`)
  bot.chat('Yuki online.')
  if (SELFTEST) runSelftest()
})

bot.on('kicked', (r) => log('kicked:', JSON.stringify(r)))
bot.on('error', (e) => log('error:', e.message))
bot.on('end', (r) => { log('disconnected:', r); process.exit(0) })
bot.on('death', () => log('died @', fmt(bot.entity.position)))
bot.on('health', () => { if (bot.health < 8) log(`health low: ${bot.health}`) })

// 聊天指令路由（后续 L2 路由层就接在这里）
bot.on('chat', async (username, message) => {
  if (username === bot.username) return
  const m = message.trim().toLowerCase()
  if (!m.startsWith('yuki')) return
  const [, cmd, ...args] = m.split(/\s+/)
  log(`<- ${username}: ${message}`)
  try {
    switch (cmd) {
      case 'come': {
        const target = bot.players[username]?.entity
        if (!target) return bot.chat(`can't see you, ${username}`)
        bot.pathfinder.setGoal(new goals.GoalNear(target.position.x, target.position.y, target.position.z, 2))
        return bot.chat('coming~')
      }
      case 'goto': {
        const [x, y, z] = args.map(Number)
        if ([x, y, z].some(Number.isNaN)) return bot.chat('usage: yuki goto x y z')
        bot.pathfinder.setGoal(new goals.GoalBlock(x, y, z))
        return bot.chat(`heading to ${x} ${y} ${z}`)
      }
      case 'follow': {
        const name = args[0] || username
        if (!bot.players[name]) return bot.chat(`no player ${name}`)
        followTarget = name
        clearInterval(followTimer)
        followTimer = setInterval(() => {
          const e = bot.players[followTarget]?.entity
          if (!e) return
          const d = bot.entity.position.distanceTo(e.position)
          if (d > 3) bot.pathfinder.setGoal(new goals.GoalNear(e.position.x, e.position.y, e.position.z, 2))
        }, 500)
        return bot.chat(`following ${name}`)
      }
      case 'stop':
        clearInterval(followTimer); followTarget = null
        bot.pathfinder.setGoal(null); bot.clearControlStates()
        return bot.chat('stopped.')
      case 'pos':
        return bot.chat(`I'm at ${fmt(bot.entity.position)}`)
      case 'status':
        return bot.chat(JSON.stringify({
          pos: fmt(bot.entity.position), hp: bot.health, food: bot.food,
          players: Object.keys(bot.players).filter((n) => n !== bot.username),
          time: bot.time.timeOfDay, pathing: !!bot.pathfinder.goal
        }))
      case 'drop':
        return bot.chat('dropping everything, bye~') || setTimeout(() => bot.quit(), 300)
      default:
        return bot.chat(`unknown cmd: ${cmd}`)
    }
  } catch (e) { log('cmd error:', e.message) }
})

// 自检：报位置 -> 走到偏移坐标 -> 校验到达
function runSelftest () {
  const start = bot.entity.position.clone()
  log('SELFTEST start', fmt(start))
  const tx = Math.round(start.x) + 4, tz = Math.round(start.z) + 4
  bot.pathfinder.setGoal(new goals.GoalBlock(tx, Math.round(start.y), tz))
  const t0 = Date.now()
  const iv = setInterval(async () => {
    const p = bot.entity.position
    if (Math.hypot(p.x - tx, p.z - tz) < 1.6) {
      clearInterval(iv)
      log(`SELFTEST OK: reached ${fmt(p)} in ${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(1)}s`)
      log('SELFTEST survival check: hp', bot.health, 'food', bot.food, 'entities', Object.keys(bot.entities).length)
      bot.chat('selftest ok')
      setTimeout(() => { bot.quit(); process.exit(0) }, 1000)
    } else if (Date.now() - t0 > 45000) {
      clearInterval(iv)
      log('SELFTEST FAIL: timeout, at', fmt(bot.entity.position), 'goal', tx, tz, 'err', bot.pathfinder.bestHarvestTool ? '' : '')
      process.exit(2)
    }
  }, 250)
}

================
yuki-bot/sidecar/bot.js
================

'use strict'
// sidecar/bot.js —— mineflayer 连接与只读状态快照。
const mineflayer = require('mineflayer')
const { pathfinder, Movements, goals } = require('mineflayer-pathfinder')
const config = require('./config')
const log = require('./log')

class BotLink {
  constructor () {
    this.bot = null
    this.mcData = null
    this.movements = null
    this.ready = false
    this.deathCount = 0
    this.lastDeathAt = 0
    this.onChat = null
  }

  async connect () {
    const bot = mineflayer.createBot({
      host: config.mc.host,
      port: config.mc.port,
      username: config.mc.username,
      version: config.mc.version,
      auth: 'offline'
    })
    this.bot = bot
    bot.loadPlugin(pathfinder)
    bot.loadPlugin(require('mineflayer-collectblock').plugin)
    bot.loadPlugin(require('mineflayer-tool').plugin)
    // pvp 必须在 pathfinder 之后加载（坑 3），加载后靠 setMovements 生效
    bot.loadPlugin(require('mineflayer-pvp').plugin)

    bot.on('kicked', (reason) => log.warn('bot_kicked', { reason: safe(reason) }))
    bot.on('error', (err) => log.error('bot_error', { message: err.message }))
    bot.on('end', (reason) => { this.ready = false; log.warn('bot_end', { reason: String(reason) }) })
    bot.on('death', () => {
      this.deathCount += 1
      this.lastDeathAt = Date.now()
      log.warn('bot_death', { pos: fmtPos(bot.entity && bot.entity.position), deaths: this.deathCount })
    })
    bot.on('chat', (username, message) => {
      if (!this.onChat) return
      if (username === bot.username) return
      this.onChat(username, message)
    })

    await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('spawn timeout')), 60000)
      bot.once('spawn', () => { clearTimeout(timer); resolve() })
      bot.once('error', (err) => { clearTimeout(timer); reject(err) })
    })

    this.mcData = require('minecraft-data')(bot.version)
    this.movements = new Movements(bot, this.mcData)
    this.movements.canDig = true
    this.movements.dontMineUnderFallingBlock = false
    this.movements.dontCreateFlow = false
    bot.pathfinder.setMovements(this.movements)
    this.ready = true
    log.info('bot_spawned', {
      username: bot.username,
      version: bot.version,
      pos: fmtPos(bot.entity.position)
    })
    return bot
  }

  /** 背包 {itemId: count}，只读。 */
  inventoryCounts () {
    const counts = {}
    if (!this.bot || !this.bot.inventory) return counts
    for (const item of this.bot.inventory.items()) {
      counts[item.type] = (counts[item.type] || 0) + item.count
    }
    return counts
  }

  inventorySummary () {
    if (!this.bot || !this.bot.inventory) return []
    const byName = {}
    for (const item of this.bot.inventory.items()) {
      byName[item.name] = (byName[item.name] || 0) + item.count
    }
    return Object.entries(byName).map(([name, count]) => ({ name, count }))
  }

  /** 附近玩家的真实游戏 ID + 坐标（L2 规划「过来/跟着我」要用，别让它猜名字）。 */
  playersNearby (maxDistance = 64) {
    const bot = this.bot
    if (!bot || !bot.entity || !bot.players) return []
    const out = []
    for (const player of Object.values(bot.players)) {
      if (!player || !player.entity || player.username === bot.username) continue
      const p = player.entity.position
      const d = bot.entity.position.distanceTo(p)
      if (d > maxDistance) continue
      out.push({
        name: player.username,
        x: Math.round(p.x),
        y: Math.round(p.y),
        z: Math.round(p.z),
        distance: round(d)
      })
    }
    return out.sort((a, b) => a.distance - b.distance).slice(0, 5)
  }

  pos () {
    const p = this.bot && this.bot.entity && this.bot.entity.position
    return p ? { x: round(p.x), y: round(p.y), z: round(p.z) } : null
  }

  /** 技能执行期的即时状态，由任务执行器写入。 */
  snapshot (runtime) {
    const bot = this.bot
    return {
      bot: this.ready ? 'ready' : 'offline',
      status: runtime.status,
      task: runtime.task || 'none',
      note: runtime.note,
      reply: runtime.reply,
      pos: this.pos(),
      time_of_day: bot && bot.time ? bot.time.timeOfDay : null,
      day: bot && bot.time ? bot.time.day : null,
      health: bot ? bot.health : null,
      food: bot ? bot.food : null,
      pathing: Boolean(bot && bot.pathfinder && bot.pathfinder.goal),
      inventory: this.inventorySummary(),
      players: this.playersNearby(),
      queue: runtime.queue || [],
      current: runtime.current || null,
      deaths: this.deathCount,
      updatedAt: Date.now()
    }
  }

  /** 硬停：清 goal、停采集、停攻击、关容器、复位控制状态。1 秒内必须生效。 */
  hardStop () {
    const bot = this.bot
    if (!bot) return
    try { bot.pathfinder.setGoal(null) } catch (_) { /* noop */ }
    try { bot.pathfinder.stop() } catch (_) { /* noop */ }
    if (bot.collectBlock) {
      try { bot.collectBlock.cancelTask() } catch (_) { /* noop */ }
    }
    if (bot.pvp) {
      try { bot.pvp.forceStop() } catch (_) { /* noop */ }
    }
    if (bot.currentWindow) {
      try { bot.closeWindow(bot.currentWindow) } catch (_) { /* noop */ }
    }
    try { bot.stopDigging() } catch (_) { /* noop */ }
    try { bot.deactivateItem() } catch (_) { /* noop */ }
    try { bot.clearControlStates() } catch (_) { /* noop */ }
  }

  /** 脚下的方块名（脚、头、下方），读不到返回 null。 */
  blocksAroundFeet () {
    const bot = this.bot
    if (!bot || !bot.entity) return {}
    const p = bot.entity.position.floored()
    const at = (dy) => {
      const b = bot.blockAt(p.offset(0, dy, 0))
      return b ? b.name : null
    }
    return { feet: at(0), head: at(1), below: at(-1) }
  }

  say (text) {
    if (!this.bot || !this.ready) return false
    const line = String(text || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 240)
    if (!line) return false
    this.bot.chat(line)
    return true
  }

  /** 静态定点寻路。注意不能传 dynamic=true：pathfinder 在动态 goal 下不发 goal_reached（坑 18）。 */
  async gotoNear (x, y, z, range) {
    const bot = this.bot
    const goal = new goals.GoalNear(Math.round(x), Math.round(y), Math.round(z), Math.max(0, range || 0))
    bot.pathfinder.setGoal(goal)
  }

  goalReached (timeoutMs, { stallMs = 10000, minMove = 0.4 } = {}) {
    const bot = this.bot
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      let done = false
      let lastPos = bot.entity ? bot.entity.position.clone() : null
      let lastMoveAt = Date.now()
      const finish = (ok, reason) => {
        if (done) return
        done = true
        clearTimeout(timer)
        clearInterval(watchdog)
        bot.removeListener('goal_reached', onReach)
        bot.removeListener('path_update', onUpdate)
        bot.removeListener('goal_updated', onGoalUpdated)
        bot.removeListener('path_stop', onPathStop)
        resolve({ ok, reason })
      }
      const onReach = () => finish(true, 'goal_reached')
      // 被别的东西改掉 goal（守护逻辑 / 新任务）也算结束，别让技能挂到超时
      const onGoalUpdated = (goal) => { if (!goal) finish(false, 'goal_cleared') }
      // pathfinder 自己 stop() 只发 path_stop，不发 goal_updated（坑 19）
      const onPathStop = () => finish(false, 'path_stopped')
      const onUpdate = (r) => {
        if (r.status === 'noPath') finish(false, 'no_path')
        else if (r.status === 'timeout') finish(false, 'path_timeout')
      }
      // 卡住不动（A* 反复 partial 但从没走过去）也要能失败退出
      const watchdog = setInterval(() => {
        if (!bot.entity) return
        const p = bot.entity.position
        if (!lastPos || p.distanceTo(lastPos) >= minMove) {
          lastPos = p.clone()
          lastMoveAt = Date.now()
        } else if (Date.now() - lastMoveAt >= stallMs) {
          finish(false, 'stalled')
        }
      }, 500)
      const timer = setTimeout(() => finish(false, 'timeout'), timeoutMs)
      bot.on('goal_reached', onReach)
      bot.on('goal_updated', onGoalUpdated)
      bot.on('path_stop', onPathStop)
      bot.on('path_update', onUpdate)
    })
  }
}

function round (n) { return Math.round(n * 10) / 10 }

function fmtPos (p) { return p ? `${round(p.x)} ${round(p.y)} ${round(p.z)}` : 'null' }

function safe (value) {
  try { return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value)) } catch (_) { return String(value) }
}

module.exports = { BotLink, fmtPos }

'use strict'
// sidecar/config.js —— 配置外置：读 configs/local.json，环境变量优先。
const fs = require('fs')
const path = require('path')

const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..')

function readLocal () {
  const file = process.env.YUKI_MC_CONFIG || path.join(ROOT, 'configs', 'local.json')
  try {
    return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8'))
  } catch (err) {
    return {}
  }
}

const raw = readLocal()
const pick = (v, d) => (v === undefined || v === null || v === '' ? d : v)
const int = (v, d) => {
  const n = parseInt(pick(v, d), 10)
  return Number.isNaN(n) ? d : n
}

const mc = raw.mc || {}
const sidecar = raw.sidecar || {}
const planner = raw.planner || {}

module.exports = {
  root: ROOT,
  mc: {
    host: pick(process.env.MC_HOST, mc.host || '127.0.0.1'),
    port: int(process.env.MC_PORT, mc.port || 25565),
    username: pick(process.env.MC_USER, mc.username || 'Yuki'),
    version: pick(process.env.MC_VERSION, mc.version || '26.1')
  },
  sidecar: {
    host: pick(process.env.SIDECAR_HOST, sidecar.host || '127.0.0.1'),
    port: int(process.env.SIDECAR_PORT, sidecar.port || 8790)
  },
  plannerUrl: pick(process.env.PLANNER_URL, planner.url || 'http://127.0.0.1:8791').replace(/\/+$/, ''),
  // 公屏转发过滤：留空 = 全部消息都进她的上下文（P3 定稿：像 chatbot 一样听所有聊天）
  chatKeyword: (process.env.SIDECAR_CHAT_KEYWORD !== undefined
    ? process.env.SIDECAR_CHAT_KEYWORD
    : (sidecar.chat_keyword !== undefined ? sidecar.chat_keyword : 'yuki')).toLowerCase(),
  limits: {
    stepTimeoutMs: int(process.env.SIDECAR_STEP_TIMEOUT_MS, 120000),
    taskTimeoutMs: int(process.env.SIDECAR_TASK_TIMEOUT_MS, 300000),
    // P2 预算：单步重试上限默认 2（PRD P2「retry 默认 2」）
    retryPerStep: int(process.env.SIDECAR_RETRY_PER_STEP, 2),
    queueLimit: 3,
    maxSteps: 5,
    chatReplyTimeoutMs: int(process.env.SIDECAR_CHAT_TIMEOUT_MS, 60000)
  }
}

================
yuki-bot/sidecar/guardian.js
================

'use strict'
// sidecar/guardian.js —— 守护逻辑（priority 9，硬编码，不经模型）。
// 触发即抢占当前任务（aborted 上报），并做一次保命动作；同一类事件有冷却，避免刷屏。
const log = require('./log')
const { sleep } = require('./util')

const TICK_MS = 250
const COOLDOWN_MS = 5000
const LOW_HEALTH = 6
const LOW_FOOD = 6
const LAVA_NAMES = new Set(['lava', 'flowing_lava'])
const HOLD_MS = 6000

class Guardian {
  constructor ({ link, runner, emit, eat }) {
    this.link = link
    this.runner = runner
    this.emit = emit || (() => {})
    this.eat = eat // 复用餐技能的吃逻辑，避免两套实现
    this.timer = null
    this.cooldown = {}
    this.lastHurtAt = 0
    this.lastAttacker = null
    this.deathAt = 0
    this.recovering = false
    this.last = null
    this.count = 0
  }

  start () {
    if (this.timer) return
    this.timer = setInterval(() => { this.tick().catch((err) => log.error('guardian_tick_failed', { error: err.message })) }, TICK_MS)
    this.tick().catch(() => {})
    const bot = this.link.bot
    if (bot) {
      bot.on('entityHurt', (entity, source) => {
        if (!bot.entity || entity !== bot.entity) return
        this.lastHurtAt = Date.now()
        this.lastAttacker = source && source.username ? source.username : (source && source.name) || null
      })
      bot.on('death', () => { this.deathAt = Date.now() })
    }
    log.info('guardian_start')
  }

  stop () {
    if (this.timer) clearInterval(this.timer)
    this.timer = null
  }

  status () {
    return { last: this.last, count: this.count, recovering: this.recovering }
  }

  async tick () {
    const link = this.link
    if (!link.ready || !link.bot || !link.bot.entity) return
    if (this.recovering) return
    if (this._cooling('any')) return

    const bot = link.bot
    const now = Date.now()

    if (now - this.deathAt < 3000 && !this._cooling('death')) {
      return this.trigger('death', 10, { pos: link.pos() }, async () => {})
    }

    const blocks = link.blocksAroundFeet()
    if (LAVA_NAMES.has(blocks.feet) || LAVA_NAMES.has(blocks.head)) {
      return this.trigger('lava', 9, { blocks }, () => this.escape())
    }
    if (bot.entity.position.y < -50) {
      return this.trigger('void', 9, { pos: link.pos() }, () => this.escape())
    }
    if (typeof bot.health === 'number' && bot.health <= LOW_HEALTH) {
      return this.trigger('low_health', 9, { health: bot.health }, () => this.survive())
    }
    // 正在主动打怪时不因受击抢占（否则 attack 技能永远打不完）；低血/岩浆仍会触发
    if (now - this.lastHurtAt < 1500 && !this._inCombat()) {
      return this.trigger('hurt', 9, { attacker: this.lastAttacker, health: bot.health }, () => this.survive())
    }
    if (typeof bot.food === 'number' && bot.food <= LOW_FOOD) {
      return this.trigger('hunger', 8, { food: bot.food }, () => this.feed())
    }
  }

  _inCombat () {
    const current = this.runner.current
    if (!current) return false
    const step = current.steps[current.stepIndex]
    return Boolean(step && step.skill === 'attack')
  }

  _cooling (kind) {
    const until = this.cooldown[kind] || 0
    return Date.now() < until
  }

  /** 抢占 + 保命动作 + 上报。 */
  async trigger (kind, severity, detail, action) {
    this.cooldown[kind] = Date.now() + COOLDOWN_MS
    this.cooldown.any = Date.now() + 1000
    this.recovering = true
    this.count += 1
    this.last = { kind, severity, at: Date.now(), detail }
    this.runner.hold(HOLD_MS)

    const preempted = this.runner.cancel(undefined, `guardian:${kind}`)
    log.warn('guardian_trigger', { kind, severity, detail, preempted })
    this.emit({ type: 'emergency', kind, severity, detail, preempted: preempted.cancelled ? preempted.intention_id : null })

    try {
      await action()
    } catch (err) {
      log.error('guardian_action_failed', { kind, error: err.message })
    } finally {
      this.runner.releaseHold()
      this.recovering = false
    }
  }

  /** 岩浆/虚空：找最近的落脚点跑过去；找不到就原地跳（岩浆里跳能上浮）。 */
  async escape () {
    const bot = this.link.bot
    if (!bot || !bot.entity) return
    this.link.hardStop()
    const from = bot.entity.position.floored()
    const spot = findSafeSpot(bot, from, 10)
    if (spot) {
      await this.link.gotoNear(spot.x, spot.y, spot.z, 0)
      const result = await this.link.goalReached(5000)
      log.info('guardian_escape', { to: spot, reached: result.ok, reason: result.reason })
    } else {
      bot.setControlState('jump', true)
      await sleep(2000)
      bot.clearControlStates()
      log.warn('guardian_escape_no_spot')
    }
    this.link.hardStop()
  }

  /** 濒死/受击：先吃一口，再从最近的敌对生物身边退开。 */
  async survive () {
    await this.feed()
    const bot = this.link.bot
    const threat = nearestHostile(bot, this.link.mcData, 10)
    if (!threat) return
    const me = bot.entity.position
    const away = me.plus(me.minus(threat.position).normalize().scaled(8))
    const spot = findSafeSpot(bot, away.floored(), 6) || away.floored()
    await this.link.gotoNear(spot.x, spot.y, spot.z, 1)
    await this.link.goalReached(4000)
    this.link.hardStop()
  }

  async feed () {
    if (typeof this.eat !== 'function') return
    const result = await this.eat(this.link)
    log.info('guardian_feed', result)
  }
}

function nearestHostile (bot, mcData, maxDistance) {
  if (!bot) return null
  return bot.nearestEntity((entity) => {
    if (entity === bot.entity) return false
    const def = mcData.entitiesByName[entity.name]
    if (!def || def.category !== 'Hostile mobs') return false
    return bot.entity.position.distanceTo(entity.position) <= maxDistance
  })
}

/** 找一个「脚下实心、身上两格空、附近没有岩浆」的位置。 */
function findSafeSpot (bot, from, radius) {
  for (let r = 1; r <= radius; r++) {
    for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) {
      for (let dz = -r; dz <= r; dz++) {
        if (Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dz)) !== r) continue
        const base = from.offset(dx, 0, dz)
        for (let dy = -2; dy <= 2; dy++) {
          const feet = base.offset(0, dy, 0)
          const below = bot.blockAt(feet.offset(0, -1, 0))
          const f = bot.blockAt(feet)
          const h = bot.blockAt(feet.offset(0, 1, 0))
          if (!below || !f || !h) continue
          if (below.boundingBox !== 'block') continue
          if (f.boundingBox !== 'empty' || h.boundingBox !== 'empty') continue
          if (LAVA_NAMES.has(below.name) || LAVA_NAMES.has(f.name)) continue
          return { x: feet.x, y: feet.y, z: feet.z }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return null
}

module.exports = { Guardian, findSafeSpot, nearestHostile }

================
yuki-bot/sidecar/index.js
================

'use strict'
// sidecar/index.js —— L3 常驻侧车：HTTP 接口 + 任务状态机 + bot 连接。
const http = require('http')
const config = require('./config')
const log = require('./log')
const { BotLink } = require('./bot')
const { TaskRunner } = require('./tasks')
const { Guardian } = require('./guardian')
const { eatSomething } = require('./skills/eat')

const link = new BotLink()
const sseClients = new Set()
const startedAt = Date.now()

function emit (event) {
  const payload = { ...event, at: Date.now() }
  const line = `data: ${JSON.stringify(payload)}\n\n`
  for (const res of sseClients) {
    try { res.write(line) } catch (_) { sseClients.delete(res) }
  }
  if (event.type === 'task_done' || event.type === 'step_done') {
    log.info('event', { type: event.type, intention_id: event.intention_id, status: event.status, ok: event.ok })
  }
  // P3：守护事件推给 L1 心智，休息时也得能被叫醒（severity≥7）
  if (event.type === 'emergency') {
    postJson(`${config.plannerUrl}/event`, {
      kind: event.kind,
      severity: event.severity,
      detail: event.detail
    }, 5000).catch((err) => log.warn('event_forward_failed', { kind: event.kind, error: err.message }))
  }
}

const runner = new TaskRunner({
  link,
  emit,
  onFinish: async ({ intention, status, summary, detail }) => {
    const res = await postJson(`${config.plannerUrl}/callback`, {
      intention_id: intention.intention_id,
      status,
      summary,
      detail: compactDetail(detail),
      origin: intention.origin
    })
    if (res && res.say) {
      link.say(res.say)
      emit({ type: 'say', text: res.say, source: 'callback' })
    }
    // 功能没做之类的补充说明（她的第二句）
    if (res && res.say_after) {
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 400))
      link.say(res.say_after)
      emit({ type: 'say', text: res.say_after, source: 'callback_notice' })
    }
  }
})

const guardian = new Guardian({ link, runner, emit, eat: eatSomething })

// ---------- 玩家公屏 -> L1 ----------
link.onChat = async (username, message) => {
  const keyword = config.chatKeyword
  // keyword 为空 = 不过滤，公屏所有消息都进她的上下文
  if (keyword && !message.toLowerCase().includes(keyword)) return
  log.info('chat_in', { player: username, text: message.slice(0, 80) })
  const text = message.replace(new RegExp(keyword, 'ig'), '').trim() || message.trim()
  emit({ type: 'chat_in', player: username, text })
  const started = Date.now()
  try {
    const res = await postJson(`${config.plannerUrl}/chat`, { player: username, text, at: Date.now() }, config.limits.chatReplyTimeoutMs)
    if (res && res.say) {
      link.say(res.say)
      emit({ type: 'say', text: res.say, source: 'chat', latency_ms: Date.now() - started })
    }
    if (res && res.say_after) {
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 400))
      link.say(res.say_after)
      emit({ type: 'say', text: res.say_after, source: 'chat_notice' })
    }
  } catch (err) {
    log.error('chat_forward_failed', { player: username, error: err.message, latency_ms: Date.now() - started })
  }
}

// ---------- HTTP ----------
const server = http.createServer(async (req, res) => {
  const url = new URL(req.url, 'http://localhost')
  try {
    if (req.method === 'GET' && url.pathname === '/health') {
      return sendJson(res, 200, { ok: true, bot: link.ready ? 'ready' : 'offline', uptime_s: Math.round((Date.now() - startedAt) / 1000) })
    }
    if (req.method === 'GET' && url.pathname === '/state') {
      return sendJson(res, 200, { ...link.snapshot(runner.snapshot()), guardian: guardian.status(), planner: config.plannerUrl })
    }
    if (req.method === 'GET' && url.pathname === '/events') {
      return openSse(req, res)
    }
    if (req.method === 'POST' && url.pathname === '/intention') {
      const body = await readJson(req)
      const result = runner.submit(body)
      return sendJson(res, result.status || (result.accepted ? 202 : 400), result)
    }
    if (req.method === 'POST' && url.pathname === '/cancel') {
      const body = await readJson(req)
      const result = runner.cancel(body.intention_id, body.reason || 'player_cancel')
      emit({ type: 'cancel', intention_id: body.intention_id, result })
      return sendJson(res, 200, result)
    }
    if (req.method === 'POST' && url.pathname === '/say') {
      const body = await readJson(req)
      const ok = link.say(body.text)
      emit({ type: 'say', text: body.text, source: body.source || 'http' })
      return sendJson(res, ok ? 200 : 503, { ok })
    }
    return sendJson(res, 404, { error: 'not_found', path: url.pathname })
  } catch (err) {
    log.error('http_error', { path: url.pathname, error: err.message })
    return sendJson(res, 500, { error: 'internal', message: err.message })
  }
})

function sendJson (res, status, body) {
  const text = JSON.stringify(body)
  res.writeHead(status, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8', 'Content-Length': Buffer.byteLength(text) })
  res.end(text)
}

function readJson (req, limit = 64 * 1024) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    let size = 0
    const chunks = []
    req.on('data', (chunk) => {
      size += chunk.length
      if (size > limit) { reject(new Error('body_too_large')); req.destroy(); return }
      chunks.push(chunk)
    })
    req.on('end', () => {
      if (!chunks.length) return resolve({})
      try { resolve(JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString('utf8'))) } catch (err) { reject(new Error('invalid_json')) }
    })
    req.on('error', reject)
  })
}

function openSse (req, res) {
  res.writeHead(200, {
    'Content-Type': 'text/event-stream; charset=utf-8',
    'Cache-Control': 'no-cache',
    Connection: 'keep-alive'
  })
  res.write(`data: ${JSON.stringify({ type: 'hello', at: Date.now() })}\n\n`)
  sseClients.add(res)
  const heartbeat = setInterval(() => {
    try { res.write(': ping\n\n') } catch (_) { /* noop */ }
  }, 15000)
  req.on('close', () => { clearInterval(heartbeat); sseClients.delete(res) })
}

async function postJson (url, body, timeoutMs = 60000) {
  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs)
  })
  const text = await res.text()
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`http_${res.status}: ${text.slice(0, 200)}`)
  return text ? JSON.parse(text) : {}
}

function compactDetail (detail) {
  // 只回传 L2/L1 需要的事实，不把整包原始数据塞进她上下文
  if (!detail || !Array.isArray(detail.results)) return {}
  return {
    failed_step: detail.failed_step,
    error: detail.error,
    results: detail.results.map((item) => ({
      skill: item.skill,
      ok: item.ok,
      args: item.args,
      mined: item.detail && item.detail.mined,
      block: item.detail && item.detail.block,
      requested: item.detail && item.detail.requested,
      distance: item.detail && item.detail.distance,
      crafted: item.detail && item.detail.crafted,
      container: item.detail && item.detail.container,
      took: item.detail && item.detail.took,
      deposited: item.detail && item.detail.deposited,
      killed: item.detail && item.detail.killed,
      item: item.detail && item.detail.item,
      food_before: item.detail && item.detail.food_before,
      food_after: item.detail && item.detail.food_after,
      missing: item.detail && item.detail.missing,
      needs: item.detail && item.detail.needs,
      attempts: item.detail && item.detail.attempts,
      path: item.detail && item.detail.path,
      at: item.detail && item.detail.at,
      error: item.detail && item.detail.error
    }))
  }
}

// ---------- 启动 ----------
async function main () {
  server.listen(config.sidecar.port, config.sidecar.host, () => {
    log.info('sidecar_listen', { url: `http://${config.sidecar.host}:${config.sidecar.port}`, planner: config.plannerUrl })
  })
  try {
    await link.connect()
    guardian.start()
    emit({ type: 'bot_ready', pos: link.pos() })
  } catch (err) {
    log.error('bot_connect_failed', { error: err.message })
    // 不退出：HTTP 仍可用于排障
  }
}

function shutdown (signal) {
  log.info('shutdown', { signal })
  try { guardian.stop() } catch (_) { /* noop */ }
  try { link.hardStop() } catch (_) { /* noop */ }
  for (const res of sseClients) { try { res.end() } catch (_) { /* noop */ } }
  if (link.bot) { try { link.bot.quit() } catch (_) { /* noop */ } }
  server.close(() => process.exit(0))
  setTimeout(() => process.exit(0), 3000)
}

process.on('SIGTERM', () => shutdown('SIGTERM'))
process.on('SIGINT', () => shutdown('SIGINT'))

main()

================
yuki-bot/sidecar/log.js
================

'use strict'
// sidecar/log.js —— 结构化日志：一行一个 JSON，方便 grep / 断言。
const LEVELS = { debug: 10, info: 20, warn: 30, error: 40 }
const MIN = LEVELS[process.env.SIDECAR_LOG_LEVEL || 'info'] || LEVELS.info

function emit (level, evt, fields) {
  if (LEVELS[level] < MIN) return
  const line = { t: new Date().toISOString(), lv: level, evt, ...(fields || {}) }
  const out = level === 'error' || level === 'warn' ? process.stderr : process.stdout
  out.write(JSON.stringify(line) + '\n')
}

module.exports = {
  debug: (evt, fields) => emit('debug', evt, fields),
  info: (evt, fields) => emit('info', evt, fields),
  warn: (evt, fields) => emit('warn', evt, fields),
  error: (evt, fields) => emit('error', evt, fields)
}
